[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/plans/dependency-automation-plan.md-110-| Baseline area | Automated update discovery | Security/support proof and expected limits |
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/plans/dependency-automation-plan.md-111-| --- | --- | --- |
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/plans/dependency-automation-plan.md-112-| Spring Boot, Cloud, Modulith, SpringDoc, Testcontainers, other Java dependencies | Renovate Gradle/version-catalog and wrapper managers in all five Java repos | Submit resolved graphs, including Framework, Security, Jackson, Tomcat and Netty, to Dependabot. A Boot PR alone does not establish that its BOM fixes every finding. Overrides require service-owner assessment. |
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/plans/dependency-automation-plan.md-113-| Frontend direct and transitive packages; later toolchain majors | Renovate npm manager, lockfile maintenance, Dockerfile and Actions managers | Dependabot alerts plus full and production-only `npm audit`. Compare advisory/package identities, not the old total of 20 findings. Preserve browser/SSR/dev-server applicability distinctions. |
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/plans/dependency-automation-plan.md:114:| go-redis and Go runtime | Renovate gomod, Dockerfile, Actions, and workspace download extraction | `govulncheck ./...` must reproduce or explain GO-2025-3540 and distinguish reachable calls from package presence. |
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/plans/dependency-automation-plan.md-115-| Redis, PostgreSQL, RabbitMQ, NGINX, Temurin, Jaeger, Swagger UI and other deployed third-party images | Dockerfile/Kubernetes/Helm-values managers plus Tilt and script extraction; digest updates | Trivy scans the exact selected digest and inventories the embedded software. A new digest does not prove the exact Redis or JDK patch or advisory fix. Missing package identification is a coverage gap. |
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/plans/dependency-automation-plan.md-116-| External Secrets, Kyverno, cert-manager, Istio, kube-prometheus-stack, Kiali | Helm datasource mapped to shell and Tilt pins; explicit chart names and registry URLs | Render selected charts and scan their actual image refs. Chart versions differ from app versions. Controller advisories may lack matching image-package metadata and must be recorded as misses. |
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/plans/dependency-automation-plan.md-117-| Prometheus, Grafana, Prometheus Operator, kube-state-metrics | Helm-values image refs plus chart updates | Scan explicitly overridden images as well as chart defaults. Updating a chart does not update an independently overridden image tag. |
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/plans/dependency-automation-plan.md-118-| K3s, Kind binary/node image, Kubernetes tooling, Gateway API, Calico, Helm, Tilt and other pinned tools | Built-in Docker/release datasources and annotated existing pins | Show supported-line patches and newer releases independently. Tool versions coupled to checked-in checksums need complete checksum changes before merge. Pod Security labels are compatibility settings, not ordinary package pins. |
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/plans/dependency-automation-plan.md-119-| Workspace base image, Node/Go, Zulu and installed packages | Dockerfile/ARG/download extraction where versions are declared | Scan a built workspace image for installed versions. Unversioned apt installs receive updates through rebuilds; Renovate cannot open a version PR for an absent patch pin. Preserve the digest-only Ubuntu base constraint. |
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/plans/dependency-automation-plan.md-120-| GitHub Actions and Python API-test dependencies | Native Actions and Python managers where constraints/lockfiles permit updates | Unbounded Python requirements are not version pins. Report their detection and installed-environment audit coverage without pretending an unbounded spec can receive routine version bumps. |
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/plans/dependency-automation-plan.md-121-| EOL decisions: Boot, Node, Go, RabbitMQ, NGINX, Kubernetes/K3s and Istio | Newer releases should be visible in the bot dashboard | Renovate release discovery, abandonment heuristics, and vulnerability scanners are not a comprehensive EOL detector. Retain a quarterly human support-policy check against upstream lifecycle sources. |

[codex] # ext-authz
[codex] 
[codex] Envoy/Istio HTTP external authorization service for Budget Analyzer.
[codex] 
[codex] The service validates browser sessions at the ingress authorization layer. It
[codex] reads the shared Redis session hash written by Session Gateway, authorizes
[codex] requests based on the session state, and returns identity headers for the
[codex] downstream API path.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Local Commands
[codex] 
[codex] ```bash
[codex] go test ./...
[codex] docker build -t ext-authz:local .
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] Run `gofmt -w *.go` before committing Go source changes.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Configuration
[codex] 
[codex] | Variable | Purpose | Default |
[codex] |----------|---------|---------|
[codex] | `HTTP_PORT` | HTTP ext_authz listener port. | `9002` |
[codex] | `HEALTH_PORT` | Health listener port. | `8090` |
[codex] | `REDIS_ADDR` | Redis host and port. | `redis.infrastructure:6379` |
[codex] | `REDIS_USERNAME` | Redis ACL username. | empty |
[codex] | `REDIS_EXT_AUTHZ_PASSWORD` | Redis ACL password for this service. | empty |
[codex] | `REDIS_TLS` | Enables TLS for Redis when set to `true`. | `false` |
[codex] | `REDIS_CA_CERT` | CA certificate path for Redis TLS. | empty |
[codex] | `SESSION_COOKIE_NAME` | Browser session cookie name. | `BA_SESSION` |
[codex] | `SESSION_KEY_PREFIX` | Redis key prefix for sessions. | `session:` |
[codex] | `LOG_FORMAT` | Logger format, `json` or `text`. | `json` |
[codex] 
[codex] `LOG_LEVEL` is also supported for local diagnostics.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Ownership
[codex] 
[codex] This repository owns the Go source, Go module metadata, Dockerfile, tests, and
[codex] service-local documentation for ext-authz.
[codex] 
[codex] The sibling `../orchestration` repository owns Kubernetes manifests, Istio
[codex] `ext_authz` wiring, Redis ACL bootstrap, network policies, production image
[codex] pinning, release metadata, and deployment documentation.
[codex] 
[codex] The shared browser session contract is documented in
[codex] `../orchestration/docs/architecture/session-edge-authorization-pattern.md`.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Release
[codex] 
[codex] Release images publish from
[codex] `.github/workflows/publish-release.yml` in this repository to
[codex] `ghcr.io/budgetanalyzer/ext-authz`.
[codex] 
[codex] The orchestration repository consumes released image digests in its production
[codex] desired state and builds the local Tilt image from `../ext-authz`. Deployment
[codex] and production runbooks live in `../orchestration/deploy/README.md` and
[codex] `../orchestration/kubernetes/production/README.md`.
[codex] # Dependency Automation
[codex] 
[codex] **Status:** Configuration prepared; administrator activation is still required.
[codex] 
[codex] This document owns the operating policy for dependency automation across the
[codex] Budget Analyzer repositories. The preserved
[codex] [September 6 dependency review](research/dependency-update-review-2026-09-06.md)
[codex] is the historical acceptance benchmark; its observed versions are not bot
[codex] targets. The executable rollout and acceptance sequence remains in the
[codex] [dependency automation plan](plans/dependency-automation-plan.md).
[codex] 
[codex] ## Ownership and policy
[codex] 
[codex] Renovate is the only service allowed to open dependency update pull requests.
[codex] Dependabot remains alert-only: keep the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts
[codex] enabled, disable Dependabot version updates and overlapping Dependabot security
[codex] update pull requests, and let Renovate consume alerts when its App permission
[codex] allows that. Never enable automerge, including for security fixes.
[codex] 
[codex] The shared policy is
[codex] [`renovate-presets/default.json`](../renovate-presets/default.json). Repository
[codex] configuration extends it and adds only repository-specific manager paths and
[codex] extraction rules. Routine pull requests are created weekly, with no more than
[codex] three open routine pull requests and two new routine pull requests per hour in
[codex] one repository. Vulnerability fixes use Renovate's dedicated alert path and an
[codex] unrestricted schedule. Major, chart, stateful, platform, and checksum-coupled
[codex] updates require Dependency Dashboard approval.
[codex] 
[codex] Patch, minor, and major proposals remain distinct. Digest refreshes must retain
[codex] an immutable digest and any image flavor suffix. Reviewers must independently
[codex] verify Linux ARM64 support. An available tag or a changed digest is not evidence
[codex] of architecture support, embedded package versions, vulnerability remediation,
[codex] or upstream support status.
[codex] 
[codex] The following stay outside Renovate ownership:
[codex] 
[codex] - historical/generated outputs and the retained stale test suites
[codex] - local `:latest` and generated `:tilt-*` images listed by the image-pinning
[codex]   inventories
[codex] - first-party production images and their synchronized manifest, inventory,
[codex]   overlay, runtime metadata, and API-docs metadata
[codex] - Pod Security compatibility labels
[codex] - arbitrary per-platform checksum calculation
[codex] 
[codex] Renovate may propose checksum-coupled tool versions, but dashboard approval and
[codex] the existing checksum verification remain mandatory. A reviewer must update the
[codex] complete checksum table; no validation may be weakened to merge the proposal.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Cost and service boundary
[codex] 
[codex] Use the open-source Renovate engine through the free Mend Renovate Community
[codex] GitHub App. Do not provision a self-hosted bot or opt into paid Merge Confidence,
[codex] enterprise scheduling, enhanced cache, a trial, or another subscription. The
[codex] selected security evidence uses GitHub's dependency graph and Dependabot alerts,
[codex] Gradle's open-source basic dependency-submission path, Trivy, `npm audit`, and
[codex] `govulncheck`.
[codex] 
[codex] GitHub Actions usage is separate from Mend hosting. Public repositories can use
[codex] standard hosted Linux runners. Before enabling automation for a private
[codex] repository, confirm its included Actions allowance and that paid overages are
[codex] disabled. Stop for user direction if required coverage needs spending or a
[codex] capability unavailable on the free offering.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Activation procedure
[codex] 
[codex] Configuration preparation does not authorize GitHub setting changes. A
[codex] repository administrator performs activation only after all rollout phases have
[codex] prepared and locally validated their files. Phases 1–11 require no credentials;
[codex] all authenticated validation and credential review belong to Phase 12. Agents
[codex] must never receive GitHub, package, registry, or Mend credentials, including in
[codex] Phase 12. The user performs authenticated operations in GitHub/Mend or their own
[codex] trusted environment and supplies sanitized evidence for agent review.
[codex] 
[codex] Missing authentication, authenticated-only API access, and unauthenticated API
[codex] rate limits are recorded as pending Phase 12 evidence; they do not block local
[codex] configuration preparation. This includes private Maven resolution, complete
[codex] remotely resolved Java snapshots, graph submission, published-preset resolution,
[codex] and hosted bot/scanner runs. Local schema validation, extraction, workflow lint,
[codex] and checks that can run without credentials remain required. Preserve actual
[codex] failures and incomplete outputs; never report a deferred check as passed or
[codex] weaken a hosted job's failure behavior. Non-authentication prerequisites and
[codex] implementation defects remain blockers under the owning repository's rules.
[codex] 
[codex] For each deferred check, record the repository, check/command, affected packages
[codex] or configurations, observed failure or reason not attempted, and the Phase 12
[codex] operator action and expected proof. Sibling phases keep this handoff in their
[codex] own docs and link here; Phase 12 consolidates it in the coverage report.
[codex] 
[codex] The Phase 12 operator sequence is:
[codex] 
[codex] 1. Confirm repository visibility, Actions billing/overage settings, and that the
[codex]    free Mend service supports every scoped repository and required authenticated
[codex]    Maven lookup within its job limits. Complete this before publication can
[codex]    trigger any prepared push workflows.
[codex] 2. Publish the orchestration repository and its shared preset before publishing
[codex]    consumer configurations.
[codex] 3. Manually dispatch
[codex]    `dependency-automation-config.yml` with `run_hosted_dry_run` enabled. Its
[codex]    GitHub-provided job token has read permissions only, Renovate runs with
[codex]    `--dry-run=full`, and the user retains control of the trigger. Preserve the
[codex]    workflow URL and confirm that the published preset resolves before proceeding;
[codex]    never copy the job token into an agent environment.
[codex] 4. Install the Renovate Community App only on the repositories listed in the
[codex]    rollout plan. Grant read access to Dependabot alerts when available.
[codex] 5. Enable the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts. Disable Dependabot version
[codex]    updates and automatic Dependabot security-update pull requests.
[codex] 6. Configure private Maven access only through Mend-supported encrypted secrets
[codex]    and host rules. Never commit a token and never use `pull_request_target` to
[codex]    expose trusted credentials to dependency branches.
[codex] 7. Run each Java repository's graph-submission workflow on its trusted default
[codex]    branch using its existing scoped package-read secrets and a separate GitHub
[codex]    job token for submission. Verify complete application/runtime/test dependency
[codex]    coverage and GitHub acceptance for every repo. A successful ordinary build,
[codex]    package-access preflight, or partial snapshot is insufficient. Preserve a
[codex]    sanitized snapshot or equivalent detailed graph evidence with the run URL and
[codex]    source revision; the orchestration-only Renovate dry run does not prove Maven
[codex]    resolution or Java submission. Resolve every other deferred authenticated
[codex]    lookup/build/scan and retain its evidence too.
[codex] 8. Run onboarding for orchestration first. Inspect the resolved configuration,
[codex]    extraction logs, dashboard, job duration, and any rate-limit or timeout
[codex]    message before enabling consumers.
[codex] 9. Record every baseline item in the coverage report as reproduced, superseded,
[codex]    false positive with evidence, or missing. Update discovery, advisory evidence,
[codex]    and lifecycle assessment are separate results.
[codex] 
[codex] The Community service's documented single concurrent organization job,
[codex] approximately four-hour scheduling, and 30-minute timeout are acceptable only if
[codex] the pilot completes in practice. A timeout or unsupported authenticated lookup
[codex] blocks broader activation; it is not a reason to omit dependencies.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Local validation and dry run
[codex] 
[codex] The focused workflow validates both JSON files with Renovate's official
[codex] `renovate-config-validator` under Node 24. Before publishing the preset, run the
[codex] same strict schema validation locally. For an unpublished-preset dry run, load
[codex] `renovate-presets/default.json` as the local self-hosted configuration, suppress
[codex] repository config discovery, and process the checkout with the local platform.
[codex] Use debug logging and do not provide a GitHub token or enable a write-capable
[codex] platform mode. Local mode proves extraction and supported lookups but does not
[codex] perform a true full dry run. Phase 12 owns the post-publication GitHub-platform
[codex] full dry run through the manually dispatched, read-only workflow; its ephemeral
[codex] job token is never provided to an agent.
[codex] 
[codex] Extraction and lookup evidence belongs in
[codex] [`docs/research/dependency-automation-coverage.md`](research/dependency-automation-coverage.md),
[codex] not in a second desired-version inventory.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Exact-image security evidence
[codex] 
[codex] `exact-image-security-evidence.yml` runs weekly and by manual dispatch. It has
[codex] no pull-request trigger and findings do not create a merge gate. The job fails
[codex] when rendering, registry resolution, database download, package inventory, or a
[codex] vulnerability scan is incomplete; vulnerability findings themselves leave the
[codex] scan successful. The final upload runs even after failure and retains evidence
[codex] for seven days without relying on SARIF hosting or a paid service.
[codex] 
[codex] The workflow calls `scripts/security/render-image-scan-inputs.sh`, which uses
[codex] the production Kustomize overlays, controller chart versions from
[codex] `deploy/scripts/lib/version-contract.sh`, their checked-in values, and the
[codex] Prometheus/Kiali post-renderers. It includes Helm-created controllers and hooks,
[codex] operator-supplied cert-manager solver and Prometheus config-reloader images,
[codex] standalone Jaeger and infrastructure images, and the Tilt Temurin, Kind, and
[codex] frontend production-smoke images. First-party service images remain under the
[codex] release workflow and are excluded from this scanner job.
[codex] 
[codex] Production/controller images are resolved and scanned as `linux/arm64`.
[codex] Local-only sources use the actual scanner-runner platform (`linux/amd64` on the
[codex] hosted job). For each deduplicated target, the artifact contains:
[codex] 
[codex] - the rendered source ref and every file that contributed it
[codex] - the registry manifest and the selected platform child digest
[codex] - a Trivy all-package JSON inventory with OS and application package classes
[codex] - a separate JSON vulnerability report plus captured stderr
[codex] - Trivy CLI/database and registry-resolver metadata, database-download logs,
[codex]   and a machine-readable completion status
[codex] 
[codex] A rendered tag without a digest is labelled `mutable` even though the job
[codex] immediately resolves and scans one exact platform digest. This preserves the
[codex] difference between repeatable evidence for that run and immutable checked-in
[codex] desired state. In particular, External Secrets chart 2.2.0 currently renders
[codex] `ghcr.io/external-secrets/external-secrets:v2.2.0`; its checked-in `image.digest`
[codex] value is not emitted by that chart version. The tag resolved to the intended
[codex] digest during Phase 2 validation, but later tag movement remains possible.
[codex] The cert-manager controller/startup refs and Istio CNI/pilot refs are likewise
[codex] tag-only chart output; only their resolved artifact for a particular run is
[codex] exact. The cert-manager ACME solver input does retain its checked-in digest.
[codex] 
[codex] The existing `deploy/scripts/render/observability.sh` and
[codex] `guardrails/verify-production-image-overlay.sh` are not CI render entry points:
[codex] they intentionally use a live server-side Helm dry run for Kiali RBAC. The
[codex] scanner invokes ordinary `helm template` with the same Kiali values and
[codex] post-renderer, so it needs no Kubernetes API or production credentials. One
[codex] known gap remains explicit: the Istio gateway chart renders `image: auto`, and
[codex] only a live Istio injector chooses the proxy image. The workflow records that
[codex] target as a known unscannable gap rather than adding a live-cluster dependency
[codex] or claiming coverage. Unexpected render, resolution, database, or scan failures
[codex] still fail the job.
[codex] 
[codex] Trivy package evidence is not a lifecycle authority and does not guarantee that
[codex] an image exposes its primary application version. The coverage report records
[codex] known Redis, Temurin, and controller-binary inventory limitations. Never infer a
[codex] Redis patch or Java CPU level from the broad source tag or from a successful OS
[codex] package scan.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Failure triage
[codex] 
[codex] - **Configuration error:** reproduce with the pinned validator version. Treat
[codex]   warnings and migrations as failures; correct the preset or repository config.
[codex] - **No extraction:** check the owning file's native manager pattern or adjacent
[codex]   annotation and compare debug logs. Do not seed the historical expected version
[codex]   into configuration.
[codex] - **No lookup or wrong package:** verify the real datasource identity, chart
[codex]   name, registry URL, tag prefix, build suffix, and flavor suffix. Record an
[codex]   unresolved standard-tool gap instead of adding a custom crawler.
[codex] - **Authentication or resolution failure:** preserve the failure. Fix encrypted
[codex]   Maven/registry access through the Phase 12 operator handoff. During Phases 1–11,
[codex]   record authentication-dependent checks as pending and continue independent local
[codex]   work. Never seek credentials in the agent environment, use Maven Local as proof
[codex]   of remote resolution, drop internal dependencies, or swallow the error. A
[codex]   resolution defect unrelated to authentication still needs investigation.
[codex] - **Finding without an update:** retain the Dependabot/scanner finding and route
[codex]   inherited or transitive remediation to the owning service. An update pull
[codex]   request is not proof that an advisory was detected.
[codex] - **CI failure on a bot branch:** determine whether the proposal needs its
[codex]   documented checksum, chart rendering, ARM64, state migration, or cross-repo
[codex]   companion review. Do not relax a guardrail or create orchestration drift.
[codex] - **Scanner database/download failure:** report the job as failed separately
[codex]   from vulnerability findings. Never turn an unavailable scan into a clean result.
[codex] 
[codex] Renovate discovery and vulnerability scanners do not comprehensively establish
[codex] end-of-life status. Owners must still perform a quarterly support-policy review
[codex] against upstream lifecycle sources.

[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-12-## Ownership and policy
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-13-
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md:14:Renovate is the only service allowed to open dependency update pull requests.
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-15-Dependabot remains alert-only: keep the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-16-enabled, disable Dependabot version updates and overlapping Dependabot security
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md:17:update pull requests, and let Renovate consume alerts when its App permission
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-18-allows that. Never enable automerge, including for security fixes.
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-19-
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-20-The shared policy is
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-21-[`renovate-presets/default.json`](../renovate-presets/default.json). Repository
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-22-configuration extends it and adds only repository-specific manager paths and
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-23-extraction rules. Routine pull requests are created weekly, with no more than
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-24-three open routine pull requests and two new routine pull requests per hour in
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md:25:one repository. Vulnerability fixes use Renovate's dedicated alert path and an
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-26-unrestricted schedule. Major, chart, stateful, platform, and checksum-coupled
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-27-updates require Dependency Dashboard approval.
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-28-
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-29-Patch, minor, and major proposals remain distinct. Digest refreshes must retain
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-30-an immutable digest and any image flavor suffix. Reviewers must independently
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-31-verify Linux ARM64 support. An available tag or a changed digest is not evidence
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-32-of architecture support, embedded package versions, vulnerability remediation,
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-33-or upstream support status.
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-34-
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md:35:The following stay outside Renovate ownership:
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-36-
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-37-- historical/generated outputs and the retained stale test suites
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-38-- local `:latest` and generated `:tilt-*` images listed by the image-pinning
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-39-  inventories
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-40-- first-party production images and their synchronized manifest, inventory,
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-41-  overlay, runtime metadata, and API-docs metadata
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-42-- Pod Security compatibility labels
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-43-- arbitrary per-platform checksum calculation
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-44-
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md:45:Renovate may propose checksum-coupled tool versions, but dashboard approval and
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-46-the existing checksum verification remain mandatory. A reviewer must update the
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-47-complete checksum table; no validation may be weakened to merge the proposal.

[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-50-
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md:51:Use the open-source Renovate engine through the free Mend Renovate Community
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-52-GitHub App. Do not provision a self-hosted bot or opt into paid Merge Confidence,
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-53-enterprise scheduling, enhanced cache, a trial, or another subscription. The
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-54-selected security evidence uses GitHub's dependency graph and Dependabot alerts,
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-55-Gradle's open-source basic dependency-submission path, Trivy, `npm audit`, and
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md:56:`govulncheck`.
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-57-
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-58-GitHub Actions usage is separate from Mend hosting. Public repositories can use
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-59-standard hosted Linux runners. Before enabling automation for a private
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-60-repository, confirm its included Actions allowance and that paid overages are
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-61-disabled. Stop for user direction if required coverage needs spending or a
[codex] --
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-64-## Activation procedure
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-65-
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-66-Configuration preparation does not authorize GitHub setting changes. A
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-67-repository administrator performs activation only after all rollout phases have
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-68-prepared and locally validated their files. Phases 1–11 require no credentials;
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md:69:all authenticated validation and credential review belong to Phase 12. Agents
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-70-must never receive GitHub, package, registry, or Mend credentials, including in
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md:71:Phase 12. The user performs authenticated operations in GitHub/Mend or their own
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-72-trusted environment and supplies sanitized evidence for agent review.
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-73-
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-74-Missing authentication, authenticated-only API access, and unauthenticated API
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md:75:rate limits are recorded as pending Phase 12 evidence; they do not block local
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-76-configuration preparation. This includes private Maven resolution, complete
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-77-remotely resolved Java snapshots, graph submission, published-preset resolution,
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-78-and hosted bot/scanner runs. Local schema validation, extraction, workflow lint,
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-79-and checks that can run without credentials remain required. Preserve actual
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-80-failures and incomplete outputs; never report a deferred check as passed or
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-81-weaken a hosted job's failure behavior. Non-authentication prerequisites and
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-82-implementation defects remain blockers under the owning repository's rules.
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-83-
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-84-For each deferred check, record the repository, check/command, affected packages
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md:85:or configurations, observed failure or reason not attempted, and the Phase 12
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-86-operator action and expected proof. Sibling phases keep this handoff in their
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md:87:own docs and link here; Phase 12 consolidates it in the coverage report.
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-88-
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md:89:The Phase 12 operator sequence is:
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-90-
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-91-1. Confirm repository visibility, Actions billing/overage settings, and that the
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-92-   free Mend service supports every scoped repository and required authenticated
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-93-   Maven lookup within its job limits. Complete this before publication can
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-94-   trigger any prepared push workflows.
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-95-2. Publish the orchestration repository and its shared preset before publishing
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-96-   consumer configurations.
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-97-3. Manually dispatch
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-98-   `dependency-automation-config.yml` with `run_hosted_dry_run` enabled. Its
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md:99:   GitHub-provided job token has read permissions only, Renovate runs with
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-100-   `--dry-run=full`, and the user retains control of the trigger. Preserve the
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-101-   workflow URL and confirm that the published preset resolves before proceeding;
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-102-   never copy the job token into an agent environment.
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md:103:4. Install the Renovate Community App only on the repositories listed in the
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-104-   rollout plan. Grant read access to Dependabot alerts when available.
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-105-5. Enable the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts. Disable Dependabot version
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-106-   updates and automatic Dependabot security-update pull requests.
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-107-6. Configure private Maven access only through Mend-supported encrypted secrets
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-108-   and host rules. Never commit a token and never use `pull_request_target` to
[codex] --
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-111-   branch using its existing scoped package-read secrets and a separate GitHub
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-112-   job token for submission. Verify complete application/runtime/test dependency
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-113-   coverage and GitHub acceptance for every repo. A successful ordinary build,
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-114-   package-access preflight, or partial snapshot is insufficient. Preserve a
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-115-   sanitized snapshot or equivalent detailed graph evidence with the run URL and
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md:116:   source revision; the orchestration-only Renovate dry run does not prove Maven
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-117-   resolution or Java submission. Resolve every other deferred authenticated
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-118-   lookup/build/scan and retain its evidence too.
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-119-8. Run onboarding for orchestration first. Inspect the resolved configuration,
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-120-   extraction logs, dashboard, job duration, and any rate-limit or timeout
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-121-   message before enabling consumers.

[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-131-## Local validation and dry run
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-132-
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md:133:The focused workflow validates both JSON files with Renovate's official
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md:134:`renovate-config-validator` under Node 24. Before publishing the preset, run the
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-135-same strict schema validation locally. For an unpublished-preset dry run, load
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-136-`renovate-presets/default.json` as the local self-hosted configuration, suppress
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-137-repository config discovery, and process the checkout with the local platform.
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-138-Use debug logging and do not provide a GitHub token or enable a write-capable
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-139-platform mode. Local mode proves extraction and supported lookups but does not
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md:140:perform a true full dry run. Phase 12 owns the post-publication GitHub-platform
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-141-full dry run through the manually dispatched, read-only workflow; its ephemeral
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-142-job token is never provided to an agent.
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-143-
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-144-Extraction and lookup evidence belongs in
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-145-[`docs/research/dependency-automation-coverage.md`](research/dependency-automation-coverage.md),
[codex] --
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-211-  into configuration.
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-212-- **No lookup or wrong package:** verify the real datasource identity, chart
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-213-  name, registry URL, tag prefix, build suffix, and flavor suffix. Record an
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-214-  unresolved standard-tool gap instead of adding a custom crawler.
[codex] /workspace/orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md-215-- **Authentication or resolution failure:** preserve the failure. Fix encrypted

[codex] Applicability caveats:
[codex] 
[codex] - The critical Vitest issue requires the Vitest UI server to be listening; it
[codex]   is a development-tool risk, not production browser code.
[codex] - The reviewed Vite 6.4.2 issue requires a network-exposed Windows dev server.
[codex]   The supported container path is Linux, but the patch is still low risk.
[codex] - Most React Router findings concern framework, SSR, manifest, or RSC modes.
[codex]   The application uses `BrowserRouter`, not those server modes. The open
[codex]   redirect advisory is the most relevant browser-side reason to update.
[codex] - React Server Component advisories do not apply because no
[codex]   `react-server-dom-*` package is installed. React 19.2.8 remains the sensible
[codex]   paired patch level.
[codex] 
[codex] ### ext-authz And go-redis
[codex] 
[codex] `govulncheck` found a reachable call path from `ext-authz` to GO-2025-3540 in
[codex] `github.com/redis/go-redis/v9` 9.7.0. The issue may cause out-of-order responses
[codex] when `CLIENT SETINFO` times out during connection establishment. The first
[codex] fixed version is 9.7.3; the current release at review time is 9.22.0.
[codex] 
[codex] Recommendation: update within go-redis v9, rerun `go mod tidy`, `go test ./...`,
[codex] `govulncheck ./...`, and the shared session-contract verifiers.
[codex] 
[codex] Advisory: [GO-2025-3540](https://pkg.go.dev/vuln/GO-2025-3540).
[codex]

[codex] created on 2026-04-30. The current Temurin 25 maintenance release is
[codex] 25.0.4.1+1.

[codex] 
[codex] ### Scope
[codex] 
[codex] Renovate config, `govulncheck` CI and local documentation.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Non-goals
[codex] 
[codex] No module/toolchain upgrades, `go mod tidy` changes, service code or deployment.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Required context
[codex] 
[codex] Read local instructions, `go.mod`, `go.sum`, Dockerfile and CI, plus the shared
[codex] preset and the GO-2025-3540 baseline finding.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Execution steps
[codex] 
[codex] 1. Add the preset consumer and verify go-redis, other modules, Go directive,
[codex]    builder/runtime images and Actions are extracted.
[codex] 2. Add official `govulncheck ./...` on trusted default-branch changes and a
[codex]    weekly/manual schedule; retain readable and machine-readable output. Make the
[codex]    scanner version itself a Renovate-managed input.
[codex] 3. Document findings versus reachable call paths and reuse existing test/build
[codex]    checks for bot PRs. If the scanner needs a newer analysis toolchain, distinguish
[codex]    that from the declared application toolchain and report any compatibility gap.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Implementation notes
[codex] 
[codex] Module vulnerability presence is not the same as the reachable call reported in
[codex] the research. Preserve call-path evidence where the tool can produce it.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Validation
[codex] 
[codex] Validate/extract the config, run actionlint and govulncheck against current source.
[codex] Compare GO-2025-3540 or its alias and confirm `go.mod`/`go.sum` are unchanged.
[codex] Authenticated module/tool/database access and GitHub lookups are pending Phase 12;
[codex] record their scope and complete the available credential-free validation.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Completion criteria
[codex] 
[codex] Module/runtime extraction and local configuration checks pass. Go vulnerability
[codex] evidence is recorded where available, with authenticated checks pending Phase 12
[codex] and toolchain or reachability limitations explicitly stated.

[codex] 5783:    "details": "A denial of service is possible from excessive resource consumption in net/http and mime/multipart.\n\nMultipart form parsing with mime/multipart.Reader.ReadForm can consume largely unlimited amounts of memory and disk files. This also affects form parsing in the net/http package with the Request methods FormFile, FormValue, ParseMultipartForm, and PostFormValue.\n\nReadForm takes a maxMemory parameter, and is documented as storing \"up to maxMemory bytes +10MB (reserved for non-file parts) in memory\". File parts which cannot be stored in memory are stored on disk in temporary files. The unconfigurable 10MB reserved for non-file parts is excessively large and can potentially open a denial of service vector on its own. However, ReadForm did not properly account for all memory consumed by a parsed form, such as map entry overhead, part names, and MIME headers, permitting a maliciously crafted form to consume well over 10MB. In addition, ReadForm contained no limit on the number of disk files created, permitting a relatively small request body to create a large number of disk temporary files.\n\nWith fix, ReadForm now properly accounts for various forms of memory overhead, and should now stay within its documented limit of 10MB + maxMemory bytes of memory consumption. Users should still be aware that this limit is high and may still be hazardous.\n\nIn addition, ReadForm now creates at most one on-disk temporary file, combining multiple form parts into a single temporary file. The mime/multipart.File interface type's documentation states, \"If stored on disk, the File's underlying concrete type will be an *os.File.\". This is no longer the case when a form contains more than one file part, due to this coalescing of parts into a single file. The previous behavior of using distinct files for each form part may be reenabled with the environment variable GODEBUG=multipartfiles=distinct.\n\nUsers should be aware that multipart.ReadForm and the http.Request methods that call it do not limit the amount of disk consumed by temporary files. [codex] 6501:    "details": "Multipart form parsing can consume large amounts of CPU and memory when processing form inputs containing very large numbers of parts.\n\nThis stems from several causes:\n\n1. mime/multipart.Reader.ReadForm limits the total memory a parsed multipart form can consume. While the total number of requests is bounded by the http2.Server.MaxConcurrentStreams setting, resetting an in-progress request allows the attacker to create a new request while the existing one is still executing.\n\nWith the fix applied, HTTP/2 servers now bound the number of simultaneously executing handler goroutines to the stream concurrency limit (MaxConcurrentStreams). New requests arriving when at the limit (which can only happen after the client has reset an existing, in-flight request) will be queued until a handler exits. [codex] +name: Go Vulnerability Check
[codex] +
[codex] +on:
[codex] +  push:
[codex] +    branches: [main]
[codex] +  schedule:
[codex] +    - cron: "17 5 * * 1"
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +
[codex] +permissions:
[codex] +  contents: read
[codex] +
[codex] +env:
[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: "true"
[codex] +  GOVULNCHECK_VERSION: "v1.7.0"
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  govulncheck:
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up analysis Go toolchain
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-go@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          go-version: "1.26.x"
[codex] +          cache: true
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Install govulncheck
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          scanner_bin="${RUNNER_TEMP}/govulncheck-bin"
[codex] +          mkdir -p "${scanner_bin}"
[codex] +          GOBIN="${scanner_bin}" GOTOOLCHAIN=local \
[codex] +            go install "golang.org/x/vuln/cmd/govulncheck@${GOVULNCHECK_VERSION}"
[codex] +          echo "${scanner_bin}" >> "${GITHUB_PATH}"
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Scan reachable Go vulnerabilities
[codex] +        id: scan
[codex] +        continue-on-error: true
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GOTOOLCHAIN: local
[codex] +        run: ./scripts/run-govulncheck.sh govulncheck-results
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Upload govulncheck evidence
[codex] +        if: always()
[codex] +        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v7
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          name: govulncheck-${{ github.sha }}
[codex] +          path: govulncheck-results/
[codex] +          if-no-files-found: error
[codex] +          retention-days: 7
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Enforce scanner completion
[codex] +        if: always()
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          SCAN_OUTCOME: ${{ steps.scan.outcome }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ "${SCAN_OUTCOME}" != "success" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "govulncheck did not complete successfully; inspect the uploaded evidence." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi

[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +**Status:** Local configuration is prepared; automation is not active.
[codex] +
[codex] +The Budget Analyzer
[codex] +[dependency automation policy](https://github.com/budgetanalyzer/orchestration/blob/main/docs/dependency-automation.md)
[codex] +owns activation, scheduling, review, and cost boundaries. Renovate is the only
[codex] +update-PR owner. Dependabot remains alert-only, and neither routine nor security
[codex] +updates are automerged.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared orchestration preset. Native Renovate managers
[codex] +cover the Go directive and direct and indirect modules in `go.mod`, both pinned
[codex] +Dockerfile images, and GitHub Actions. A repository-specific regex manager covers
[codex] +the `GOVULNCHECK_VERSION` value in
[codex] +`.github/workflows/go-vulnerability-check.yml` as the Go module
[codex] +`golang.org/x/vuln`.
[codex] +
[codex] +The existing `build.yml` workflow remains the pull-request check for formatting,
[codex] +tests, and the service image build. Dependency automation does not bypass those
[codex] +checks. The shared preset must be published before this consumer configuration is
[codex] +activated. This onboarding does not update `go.mod`, `go.sum`, the Go directive,
[codex] +container images, Actions, or application code.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Reachable vulnerability evidence
[codex] +
[codex] +`go-vulnerability-check.yml` runs after changes reach `main`, every Monday, and
[codex] +on manual dispatch. It installs the pinned official `govulncheck` CLI and runs
[codex] +both `govulncheck ./...` and `govulncheck -json ./...`. The workflow keeps the
[codex] +readable report, JSON event stream, stderr, scanner version, and exit-code summary
[codex] +for seven days.
[codex] +
[codex] +Reachable findings are reported and uploaded without failing unrelated work.
[codex] +Scanner installation, package loading, vulnerability database access, analysis,
[codex] +JSON generation, or artifact upload failures still fail the job. In particular,
[codex] +the text command's exit code 3 means findings, while any other unexpected text
[codex] +status or any nonzero JSON status is an operational error.
[codex] +
[codex] +The application still declares Go 1.24. The current pinned scanner,
[codex] +`golang.org/x/vuln` v1.7.0, requires Go 1.25 or newer, so this workflow uses Go
[codex] +1.26.x solely as its analysis toolchain and sets `GOTOOLCHAIN=local` to prevent an
[codex] +implicit toolchain change. That does not upgrade the application toolchain or its
[codex] +builder image.
[codex] +
[codex] +### Local baseline evidence
[codex] +
[codex] +On 2026-09-06, govulncheck v1.7.0 running with Go 1.26.8 reproduced
[codex] +GO-2025-3540 as a reachable vulnerability in
[codex] +`github.com/redis/go-redis/v9` 9.7.0. The fixed version reported by the scanner is
[codex] +9.7.3. A representative trace is:
[codex] +
[codex] +```text
[codex] +session.go:81:24: ext.NewSessionStore calls redis.cmdable.Ping,
[codex] +which eventually calls redis.baseClient.initConn
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +The scanner reported one reachable vulnerability from one module and no other
[codex] +vulnerabilities in imported packages or required modules. This call-path result
[codex] +is stronger evidence than module presence alone and reproduces the preserved
[codex] +September 6 review. No remediation upgrade is part of this onboarding phase.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the same local check with an explicit analysis toolchain and pinned scanner:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +GOBIN="$(mktemp -d)" GOTOOLCHAIN=local \
[codex] +  go install golang.org/x/vuln/cmd/govulncheck@v1.7.0
[codex] +./scripts/run-govulncheck.sh govulncheck-results
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +The temporary `GOBIN` must be added to `PATH` before running the script when it
[codex] +is not already part of the shell path.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 operator handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +The following evidence remains pending because this phase neither publishes
[codex] +configuration nor uses credentials:
[codex] +
[codex] +- Repository: `budgetanalyzer/ext-authz`. Check: a hosted Renovate full dry run
[codex] +  after the shared preset is published. Scope: `go.mod`, both Dockerfile images,
[codex] +  all workflow Actions and Go inputs, and the govulncheck pin. Reason: published
[codex] +  preset resolution and GitHub lookups require the Phase 12 operator context.
[codex] +  Action: run the trusted read-only hosted validation and then onboard the free
[codex] +  Renovate App. Proof: preserve the run URL and logs showing resolved policy,
[codex] +  extracted package identities, successful lookups, and no fatal/error output.
[codex] +- Repository: `budgetanalyzer/ext-authz`. Check: manually dispatch
[codex] +  `go-vulnerability-check.yml` from the trusted default branch. Scope:
[codex] +  `github.com/redis/go-redis/v9` 9.7.0, the Go module graph, scanner module, and
[codex] +  vulnerability database. Reason: the workflow is not published or activated
[codex] +  during local preparation. Action: dispatch it without exposing its ephemeral
[codex] +  GitHub token. Proof: preserve the run URL, source revision, and seven-day
[codex] +  artifact containing complete readable and JSON output; until remediation, it
[codex] +  should retain the GO-2025-3540 call path above.
[codex] +- Repository: `budgetanalyzer/ext-authz`. Check: GitHub dependency graph and
[codex] +  Dependabot alert status. Scope: the Go module graph and vulnerability alerts.
[codex] +  Reason: repository settings are administrator-owned. Action: enable the graph
[codex] +  and alerts, keep Dependabot version updates and overlapping security-update PRs
[codex] +  disabled, and grant Renovate alert-read access. Proof: sanitized settings and
[codex] +  alert evidence tied to this repository.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate discovery, Dependabot alerts, and govulncheck do not prove support
[codex] +lifecycle status. Go support remains part of the quarterly upstream lifecycle
[codex] +review, and the application Go 1.24 migration requires separate coordination.

[codex] new file mode 100644
[codex] index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..daf94a7ddb1abe8730ccdefd8acbf3d8e6cf252e
[codex] --- /dev/null
[codex] +++ b/scripts/run-govulncheck.sh
[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,56 @@
[codex] +#!/usr/bin/env bash
[codex] +
[codex] +set -uo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +output_dir="${1:-govulncheck-results}"
[codex] +mkdir -p "${output_dir}"
[codex] +
[codex] +govulncheck -version >"${output_dir}/govulncheck-version.txt" 2>&1
[codex] +version_status=$?
[codex] +
[codex] +govulncheck ./... >"${output_dir}/govulncheck.txt" \
[codex] +  2>"${output_dir}/govulncheck-text.stderr"
[codex] +text_status=$?
[codex] +
[codex] +govulncheck -json ./... >"${output_dir}/govulncheck.json" \
[codex] +  2>"${output_dir}/govulncheck-json.stderr"
[codex] +json_status=$?
[codex] +
[codex] +cat "${output_dir}/govulncheck.txt"
[codex] +cat "${output_dir}/govulncheck-text.stderr" >&2
[codex] +cat "${output_dir}/govulncheck-json.stderr" >&2
[codex] +
[codex] +result="operational-error"
[codex] +if [[ "${version_status}" -eq 0 && "${json_status}" -eq 0 ]]; then
[codex] +  case "${text_status}" in
[codex] +    0)
[codex] +      result="clean"
[codex] +      ;;
[codex] +    3)
[codex] +      result="findings"
[codex] +      ;;
[codex] +  esac
[codex] +fi
[codex] +
[codex] +printf '{\n  "result": "%s",\n  "version_exit_code": %d,\n  "text_exit_code": %d,\n  "json_exit_code": %d\n}\n' \
[codex] +  "${result}" "${version_status}" "${text_status}" "${json_status}" \
[codex] +  >"${output_dir}/scan-result.json"
[codex] +
[codex] +if [[ -n "${GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY:-}" ]]; then
[codex] +  {
[codex] +    echo "## govulncheck"
[codex] +    echo
[codex] +    echo "Result: ${result}"
[codex] +    echo
[codex] +    echo "Text exit code: ${text_status}; JSON exit code: ${json_status}."
[codex] +  } >>"${GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY}"
[codex] +fi
[codex] +
[codex] +if [[ "${result}" == "operational-error" ]]; then
[codex] +  echo "govulncheck failed to produce complete text and JSON evidence." >&2
[codex] +  exit 1
[codex] +fi
[codex] +
[codex] +if [[ "${result}" == "findings" ]]; then
[codex] +  echo "govulncheck reported reachable vulnerabilities; findings are recorded but are not a merge gate." >&2
[codex] +fi
[codex]

[codex] +  schedule:
[codex] +    - cron: "17 5 * * 1"
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +

[codex] +  contents: read
[codex] +

[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: "true"
[codex] +  GOVULNCHECK_VERSION: "v1.7.0"
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  govulncheck:
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up analysis Go toolchain
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-go@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          go-version: "1.26.x"
[codex] +          cache: true
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Install govulncheck
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail

[codex] +            go install "golang.org/x/vuln/cmd/govulncheck@${GOVULNCHECK_VERSION}"
[codex] +          echo "${scanner_bin}" >> "${GITHUB_PATH}"
[codex] +

[codex] +        id: scan
[codex] +        continue-on-error: true

[codex] +        run: ./scripts/run-govulncheck.sh govulncheck-results

[codex] +          path: govulncheck-results/

[codex] +          retention-days: 7
[codex] +

[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +

[codex] +            echo "govulncheck did not complete successfully; inspect the uploaded evidence." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi

[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +**Status:** Local configuration is prepared; automation is not active.
[codex] +
[codex] +The Budget Analyzer
[codex] +[dependency automation policy](https://github.com/budgetanalyzer/orchestration/blob/main/docs/dependency-automation.md)
[codex] +owns activation, scheduling, review, and cost boundaries. Renovate is the only
[codex] +update-PR owner. Dependabot remains alert-only, and neither routine nor security
[codex] +updates are automerged.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared orchestration preset. Native Renovate managers
[codex] +cover the Go directive and direct and indirect modules in `go.mod`, both pinned
[codex] +Dockerfile images, and GitHub Actions. A repository-specific regex manager covers
[codex] +the `GOVULNCHECK_VERSION` value in
[codex] +`.github/workflows/go-vulnerability-check.yml` as the Go module
[codex] +`golang.org/x/vuln`.
[codex] +
[codex] +The existing `build.yml` workflow remains the pull-request check for formatting,
[codex] +tests, and the service image build. Dependency automation does not bypass those
[codex] +checks. The shared preset must be published before this consumer configuration is
[codex] +activated. This onboarding does not update `go.mod`, `go.sum`, the Go directive,
[codex] +container images, Actions, or application code.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Reachable vulnerability evidence
[codex] +
[codex] +`go-vulnerability-check.yml` runs after changes reach `main`, every Monday, and
[codex] +on manual dispatch. It installs the pinned official `govulncheck` CLI and runs
[codex] +both `govulncheck ./...` and `govulncheck -json ./...`. The workflow keeps the
[codex] +readable report, JSON event stream, stderr, scanner version, and exit-code summary
[codex] +for seven days.
[codex] +
[codex] +Reachable findings are reported and uploaded without failing unrelated work.
[codex] +Scanner installation, package loading, vulnerability database access, analysis,
[codex] +JSON generation, or artifact upload failures still fail the job. In particular,
[codex] +the text command's exit code 3 means findings, while any other unexpected text
[codex] +status or any nonzero JSON status is an operational error.
[codex] +
[codex] +The application still declares Go 1.24. The current pinned scanner,
[codex] +`golang.org/x/vuln` v1.7.0, requires Go 1.25 or newer, so this workflow uses Go
[codex] +1.26.x solely as its analysis toolchain and sets `GOTOOLCHAIN=local` to prevent an
[codex] +implicit toolchain change. That does not upgrade the application toolchain or its
[codex] +builder image.
[codex] +
[codex] +### Local baseline evidence
[codex] +
[codex] +On 2026-09-06, govulncheck v1.7.0 running with Go 1.26.8 reproduced
[codex] +GO-2025-3540 as a reachable vulnerability in
[codex] +`github.com/redis/go-redis/v9` 9.7.0. The fixed version reported by the scanner is
[codex] +9.7.3. A representative trace is:
[codex] +
[codex] +```text
[codex] +session.go:81:24: ext.NewSessionStore calls redis.cmdable.Ping,
[codex] +which eventually calls redis.baseClient.initConn
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +The scanner reported one reachable vulnerability from one module and no other
[codex] +vulnerabilities in imported packages or required modules. This call-path result
[codex] +is stronger evidence than module presence alone and reproduces the preserved
[codex] +September 6 review. No remediation upgrade is part of this onboarding phase.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the same local check with an explicit analysis toolchain and pinned scanner:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +GOBIN="$(mktemp -d)" GOTOOLCHAIN=local \
[codex] +  go install golang.org/x/vuln/cmd/govulncheck@v1.7.0
[codex] +./scripts/run-govulncheck.sh govulncheck-results
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +The temporary `GOBIN` must be added to `PATH` before running the script when it
[codex] +is not already part of the shell path.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 operator handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +The following evidence remains pending because this phase neither publishes
[codex] +configuration nor uses credentials:
[codex] +
[codex] +- Repository: `budgetanalyzer/ext-authz`. Check: a hosted Renovate full dry run
[codex] +  after the shared preset is published. Scope: `go.mod`, both Dockerfile images,
[codex] +  all workflow Actions and Go inputs, and the govulncheck pin. Reason: published
[codex] +  preset resolution and GitHub lookups require the Phase 12 operator context.
[codex] +  Action: run the trusted read-only hosted validation and then onboard the free
[codex] +  Renovate App. Proof: preserve the run URL and logs showing resolved policy,
[codex] +  extracted package identities, successful lookups, and no fatal/error output.
[codex] +- Repository: `budgetanalyzer/ext-authz`. Check: manually dispatch
[codex] +  `go-vulnerability-check.yml` from the trusted default branch. Scope:
[codex] +  `github.com/redis/go-redis/v9` 9.7.0, the Go module graph, scanner module, and
[codex] +  vulnerability database. Reason: the workflow is not published or activated
[codex] +  during local preparation. Action: dispatch it without exposing its ephemeral
[codex] +  GitHub token. Proof: preserve the run URL, source revision, and seven-day
[codex] +  artifact containing complete readable and JSON output; until remediation, it
[codex] +  should retain the GO-2025-3540 call path above.
[codex] +- Repository: `budgetanalyzer/ext-authz`. Check: GitHub dependency graph and
[codex] +  Dependabot alert status. Scope: the Go module graph and vulnerability alerts.
[codex] +  Reason: repository settings are administrator-owned. Action: enable the graph
[codex] +  and alerts, keep Dependabot version updates and overlapping security-update PRs
[codex] +  disabled, and grant Renovate alert-read access. Proof: sanitized settings and
[codex] +  alert evidence tied to this repository.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate discovery, Dependabot alerts, and govulncheck do not prove support
[codex] +lifecycle status. Go support remains part of the quarterly upstream lifecycle
[codex] +review, and the application Go 1.24 migration requires separate coordination.

[codex] new file mode 100644
[codex] index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..daf94a7ddb1abe8730ccdefd8acbf3d8e6cf252e
[codex] --- /dev/null
[codex] +++ b/scripts/run-govulncheck.sh
[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,56 @@
[codex] +#!/usr/bin/env bash
[codex] +
[codex] +set -uo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +output_dir="${1:-govulncheck-results}"
[codex] +mkdir -p "${output_dir}"
[codex] +
[codex] +govulncheck -version >"${output_dir}/govulncheck-version.txt" 2>&1
[codex] +version_status=$?
[codex] +
[codex] +govulncheck ./... >"${output_dir}/govulncheck.txt" \
[codex] +  2>"${output_dir}/govulncheck-text.stderr"
[codex] +text_status=$?
[codex] +
[codex] +govulncheck -json ./... >"${output_dir}/govulncheck.json" \
[codex] +  2>"${output_dir}/govulncheck-json.stderr"
[codex] +json_status=$?
[codex] +
[codex] +cat "${output_dir}/govulncheck.txt"
[codex] +cat "${output_dir}/govulncheck-text.stderr" >&2
[codex] +cat "${output_dir}/govulncheck-json.stderr" >&2
[codex] +
[codex] +result="operational-error"
[codex] +if [[ "${version_status}" -eq 0 && "${json_status}" -eq 0 ]]; then
[codex] +  case "${text_status}" in
[codex] +    0)
[codex] +      result="clean"
[codex] +      ;;
[codex] +    3)
[codex] +      result="findings"
[codex] +      ;;
[codex] +  esac
[codex] +fi
[codex] +
[codex] +printf '{\n  "result": "%s",\n  "version_exit_code": %d,\n  "text_exit_code": %d,\n  "json_exit_code": %d\n}\n' \
[codex] +  "${result}" "${version_status}" "${text_status}" "${json_status}" \
[codex] +  >"${output_dir}/scan-result.json"
[codex] +
[codex] +if [[ -n "${GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY:-}" ]]; then
[codex] +  {
[codex] +    echo "## govulncheck"
[codex] +    echo
[codex] +    echo "Result: ${result}"
[codex] +    echo
[codex] +    echo "Text exit code: ${text_status}; JSON exit code: ${json_status}."
[codex] +  } >>"${GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY}"
[codex] +fi
[codex] +
[codex] +if [[ "${result}" == "operational-error" ]]; then
[codex] +  echo "govulncheck failed to produce complete text and JSON evidence." >&2
[codex] +  exit 1
[codex] +fi
[codex] +
[codex] +if [[ "${result}" == "findings" ]]; then
[codex] +  echo "govulncheck reported reachable vulnerabilities; findings are recorded but are not a merge gate." >&2
[codex] +fi
[codex] 
[codex] exec
[codex] /bin/bash -lc 'chmod +x scripts/run-govulncheck.sh
[codex] 
[codex] jq empty renovate.json
[codex] bash -n scripts/run-govulncheck.sh
[codex] actionlint .github/workflows/build.yml .github/workflows/publish-release.yml .github/workflows/go-vulnerability-check.yml

[codex] +#!/usr/bin/env bash
[codex] +
[codex] +set -uo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +output_dir="${1:-govulncheck-results}"
[codex] +mkdir -p "${output_dir}"
[codex] +
[codex] +govulncheck -version >"${output_dir}/govulncheck-version.txt" 2>&1
[codex] +version_status=$?
[codex] +
[codex] +govulncheck ./... >"${output_dir}/govulncheck.txt" \
[codex] +  2>"${output_dir}/govulncheck-text.stderr"
[codex] +text_status=$?
[codex] +
[codex] +govulncheck -json ./... >"${output_dir}/govulncheck.json" \
[codex] +  2>"${output_dir}/govulncheck-json.stderr"
[codex] +json_status=$?
[codex] +
[codex] +cat "${output_dir}/govulncheck.txt"
[codex] +cat "${output_dir}/govulncheck-text.stderr" >&2
[codex] +cat "${output_dir}/govulncheck-json.stderr" >&2
[codex] +
[codex] +result="operational-error"
[codex] +if [[ "${version_status}" -eq 0 && "${json_status}" -eq 0 ]]; then
[codex] +  case "${text_status}" in
[codex] +    0)
[codex] +      result="clean"
[codex] +      ;;
[codex] +    3)
[codex] +      result="findings"
[codex] +      ;;
[codex] +  esac
[codex] +fi
[codex] +
[codex] +printf '{\n  "result": "%s",\n  "version_exit_code": %d,\n  "text_exit_code": %d,\n  "json_exit_code": %d\n}\n' \
[codex] +  "${result}" "${version_status}" "${text_status}" "${json_status}" \
[codex] +  >"${output_dir}/scan-result.json"
[codex] +
[codex] +if [[ -n "${GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY:-}" ]]; then
[codex] +  {
[codex] +    echo "## govulncheck"
[codex] +    echo
[codex] +    echo "Result: ${result}"
[codex] +    echo
[codex] +    echo "Text exit code: ${text_status}; JSON exit code: ${json_status}."
[codex] +  } >>"${GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY}"
[codex] +fi
[codex] +
[codex] +if [[ "${result}" == "operational-error" ]]; then
[codex] +  echo "govulncheck failed to produce complete text and JSON evidence." >&2
[codex] +  exit 1
[codex] +fi
[codex] +
[codex] +if [[ "${result}" == "findings" ]]; then
[codex] +  echo "govulncheck reported reachable vulnerabilities; findings are recorded but are not a merge gate." >&2
[codex] +fi
[codex]

[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +**Status:** Local configuration is prepared; automation is not active.
[codex] +
[codex] +The Budget Analyzer
[codex] +[dependency automation policy](https://github.com/budgetanalyzer/orchestration/blob/main/docs/dependency-automation.md)
[codex] +owns activation, scheduling, review, and cost boundaries. Renovate is the only
[codex] +update-PR owner. Dependabot remains alert-only, and neither routine nor security
[codex] +updates are automerged.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared orchestration preset. Native Renovate managers
[codex] +cover the Go directive and direct and indirect modules in `go.mod`, both pinned
[codex] +Dockerfile images, and GitHub Actions. A repository-specific regex manager covers
[codex] +the `GOVULNCHECK_VERSION` value in
[codex] +`.github/workflows/go-vulnerability-check.yml` as the Go module
[codex] +`golang.org/x/vuln`.
[codex] +
[codex] +The existing `build.yml` workflow remains the pull-request check for formatting,
[codex] +tests, and the service image build. Dependency automation does not bypass those
[codex] +checks. The shared preset must be published before this consumer configuration is
[codex] +activated. This onboarding does not update `go.mod`, `go.sum`, the Go directive,
[codex] +container images, Actions, or application code.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Reachable vulnerability evidence
[codex] +
[codex] +`go-vulnerability-check.yml` runs after changes reach `main`, every Monday, and
[codex] +on manual dispatch. It installs the pinned official `govulncheck` CLI and runs
[codex] +both `govulncheck ./...` and `govulncheck -json ./...`. The workflow keeps the
[codex] +readable report, JSON event stream, stderr, scanner version, and exit-code summary
[codex] +for seven days.
[codex] +
[codex] +Reachable findings are reported and uploaded without failing unrelated work.
[codex] +Scanner installation, package loading, vulnerability database access, analysis,
[codex] +JSON generation, or artifact upload failures still fail the job. In particular,
[codex] +the text command's exit code 3 means findings, while any other unexpected text
[codex] +status or any nonzero JSON status is an operational error.
[codex] +
[codex] +The application still declares Go 1.24. The current pinned scanner,
[codex] +`golang.org/x/vuln` v1.7.0, requires Go 1.25 or newer, so this workflow uses Go
[codex] +1.26.x solely as its analysis toolchain and sets `GOTOOLCHAIN=local` to prevent an
[codex] +implicit toolchain change. That does not upgrade the application toolchain or its
[codex] +builder image.
[codex] +
[codex] +### Local baseline evidence
[codex] +
[codex] +On 2026-09-06, govulncheck v1.7.0 running with Go 1.26.8 reproduced
[codex] +GO-2025-3540 as a reachable vulnerability in
[codex] +`github.com/redis/go-redis/v9` 9.7.0. The fixed version reported by the scanner is
[codex] +9.7.3. A representative trace is:
[codex] +
[codex] +```text
[codex] +session.go:81:24: ext.NewSessionStore calls redis.cmdable.Ping,
[codex] +which eventually calls redis.baseClient.initConn
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +The scanner reported one reachable vulnerability from one module and no other
[codex] +vulnerabilities in imported packages or required modules. This call-path result
[codex] +is stronger evidence than module presence alone and reproduces the preserved
[codex] +September 6 review. No remediation upgrade is part of this onboarding phase.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the same local check with an explicit analysis toolchain and pinned scanner:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +GOBIN="$(mktemp -d)" GOTOOLCHAIN=local \
[codex] +  go install golang.org/x/vuln/cmd/govulncheck@v1.7.0
[codex] +./scripts/run-govulncheck.sh govulncheck-results
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +The temporary `GOBIN` must be added to `PATH` before running the script when it
[codex] +is not already part of the shell path.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 operator handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +The following evidence remains pending because this phase neither publishes

[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6

[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-go@v6

[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,103 @@
[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +**Status:** Local configuration is prepared; automation is not active.
[codex] +
[codex] +The Budget Analyzer
[codex] +[dependency automation policy](https://github.com/budgetanalyzer/orchestration/blob/main/docs/dependency-automation.md)
[codex] +owns activation, scheduling, review, and cost boundaries. Renovate is the only
[codex] +update-PR owner. Dependabot remains alert-only, and neither routine nor security
[codex] +updates are automerged.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared orchestration preset. Native Renovate managers
[codex] +cover the Go directive and direct and indirect modules in `go.mod`, both pinned
[codex] +Dockerfile images, and GitHub Actions. A repository-specific regex manager covers
[codex] +the `GOVULNCHECK_VERSION` value in
[codex] +`.github/workflows/go-vulnerability-check.yml` as the Go module
[codex] +`golang.org/x/vuln`.
[codex] +
[codex] +The existing `build.yml` workflow remains the pull-request check for formatting,
[codex] +tests, and the service image build. Dependency automation does not bypass those
[codex] +checks. The shared preset must be published before this consumer configuration is
[codex] +activated. This onboarding does not update `go.mod`, `go.sum`, the Go directive,
[codex] +container images, Actions, or application code.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Reachable vulnerability evidence
[codex] +
[codex] +`go-vulnerability-check.yml` runs after changes reach `main`, every Monday, and
[codex] +on manual dispatch. It installs the pinned official `govulncheck` CLI and runs
[codex] +both `govulncheck ./...` and `govulncheck -json ./...`. The workflow keeps the
[codex] +readable report, JSON event stream, stderr, scanner version, and exit-code summary
[codex] +for seven days.
[codex] +
[codex] +Reachable findings are reported and uploaded without failing unrelated work.
[codex] +Scanner installation, package loading, vulnerability database access, analysis,
[codex] +JSON generation, or artifact upload failures still fail the job. In particular,
[codex] +the text command's exit code 3 means findings, while any other unexpected text
[codex] +status or any nonzero JSON status is an operational error.
[codex] +
[codex] +The application still declares Go 1.24. The current pinned scanner,
[codex] +`golang.org/x/vuln` v1.7.0, requires Go 1.25 or newer, so this workflow uses Go
[codex] +1.26.x solely as its analysis toolchain and sets `GOTOOLCHAIN=local` to prevent an
[codex] +implicit toolchain change. That does not upgrade the application toolchain or its
[codex] +builder image.
[codex] +
[codex] +### Local baseline evidence
[codex] +
[codex] +On 2026-09-06, govulncheck v1.7.0 running with Go 1.26.8 reproduced
[codex] +GO-2025-3540 as a reachable vulnerability in
[codex] +`github.com/redis/go-redis/v9` 9.7.0. The fixed version reported by the scanner is
[codex] +9.7.3. A representative trace is:
[codex] +
[codex] +```text
[codex] +session.go:81:24: ext.NewSessionStore calls redis.cmdable.Ping,
[codex] +which eventually calls redis.baseClient.initConn
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +The scanner reported one reachable vulnerability from one module and no other
[codex] +vulnerabilities in imported packages or required modules. This call-path result
[codex] +is stronger evidence than module presence alone and reproduces the preserved
[codex] +September 6 review. No remediation upgrade is part of this onboarding phase.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the same local check with an explicit analysis toolchain and pinned scanner:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +GOBIN="$(mktemp -d)" GOTOOLCHAIN=local \
[codex] +  go install golang.org/x/vuln/cmd/govulncheck@v1.7.0
[codex] +./scripts/run-govulncheck.sh govulncheck-results
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +The temporary `GOBIN` must be added to `PATH` before running the script when it
[codex] +is not already part of the shell path.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 operator handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +The following evidence remains pending because this phase neither publishes
[codex] +configuration nor uses credentials:
[codex] +
[codex] +- Repository: `budgetanalyzer/ext-authz`. Check: a hosted Renovate full dry run
[codex] +  after the shared preset is published. Scope: `go.mod`, both Dockerfile images,
[codex] +  all workflow Actions and Go inputs, and the govulncheck pin. Reason: published
[codex] +  preset resolution and GitHub lookups require the Phase 12 operator context.
[codex] +  Action: run the trusted read-only hosted validation and then onboard the free
[codex] +  Renovate App. Proof: preserve the run URL and logs showing resolved policy,
[codex] +  extracted package identities, successful lookups, and no fatal/error output.
[codex] +- Repository: `budgetanalyzer/ext-authz`. Check: manually dispatch
[codex] +  `go-vulnerability-check.yml` from the trusted default branch. Scope:
[codex] +  `github.com/redis/go-redis/v9` 9.7.0, the Go module graph, scanner module, and
[codex] +  vulnerability database. Reason: the workflow is not published or activated
[codex] +  during local preparation. Action: dispatch it without exposing its ephemeral
[codex] +  GitHub token. Proof: preserve the run URL, source revision, and seven-day
[codex] +  artifact containing complete readable and JSON output; until remediation, it
[codex] +  should retain the GO-2025-3540 call path above.
[codex] +- Repository: `budgetanalyzer/ext-authz`. Check: GitHub dependency graph and
[codex] +  Dependabot alert status. Scope: the Go module graph and vulnerability alerts.
[codex] +  Reason: repository settings are administrator-owned. Action: enable the graph
[codex] +  and alerts, keep Dependabot version updates and overlapping security-update PRs
[codex] +  disabled, and grant Renovate alert-read access. Proof: sanitized settings and
[codex] +  alert evidence tied to this repository.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate discovery, Dependabot alerts, and govulncheck do not prove support
[codex] +lifecycle status. Go support remains part of the quarterly upstream lifecycle
[codex] +review, and the application Go 1.24 migration requires separate coordination.
