[codex] +
[codex] +output_dir="${1:-govulncheck-results}"
[codex] +mkdir -p "${output_dir}"
[codex] +
[codex] +govulncheck -version >"${output_dir}/govulncheck-version.txt" 2>&1
[codex] +version_status=$?
[codex] +
[codex] +govulncheck ./... >"${output_dir}/govulncheck.txt" \
[codex] +  2>"${output_dir}/govulncheck-text.stderr"
[codex] +text_status=$?
[codex] +
[codex] +govulncheck -json ./... >"${output_dir}/govulncheck.json" \
[codex] +  2>"${output_dir}/govulncheck-json.stderr"
[codex] +json_status=$?
[codex] +
[codex] +json_validation_status=1
[codex] +if [[ -s "${output_dir}/govulncheck.json" ]]; then
[codex] +  jq -e . "${output_dir}/govulncheck.json" >/dev/null \
[codex] +    2>"${output_dir}/govulncheck-json-validation.stderr"
[codex] +  json_validation_status=$?
[codex] +else
[codex] +  echo "govulncheck JSON output is empty." \
[codex] +    >"${output_dir}/govulncheck-json-validation.stderr"
[codex] +fi
[codex] +
[codex] +evidence_status=0
[codex] +for evidence_file in govulncheck-version.txt govulncheck.txt; do
[codex] +  if [[ ! -s "${output_dir}/${evidence_file}" ]]; then
[codex] +    echo "Required evidence is empty: ${evidence_file}" >&2
[codex] +    evidence_status=1
[codex] +  fi
[codex] +done
[codex] +
[codex] +cat "${output_dir}/govulncheck.txt"
[codex] +cat "${output_dir}/govulncheck-text.stderr" >&2
[codex] +cat "${output_dir}/govulncheck-json.stderr" >&2
[codex] +
[codex] +result="operational-error"
[codex] +if [[ "${version_status}" -eq 0 && "${json_status}" -eq 0 && \
[codex] +  "${json_validation_status}" -eq 0 && "${evidence_status}" -eq 0 ]]; then
[codex] +  case "${text_status}" in
[codex] +    0)
[codex] +      result="clean"
[codex] +      ;;
[codex] +    3)
[codex] +      result="findings"
[codex] +      ;;
[codex] +  esac
[codex] +fi
[codex] +
[codex] +printf '{\n  "result": "%s",\n  "version_exit_code": %d,\n  "text_exit_code": %d,\n  "json_exit_code": %d,\n  "json_validation_exit_code": %d,\n  "evidence_exit_code": %d\n}\n' \
[codex] +  "${result}" "${version_status}" "${text_status}" "${json_status}" \
[codex] +  "${json_validation_status}" "${evidence_status}" \
[codex] +  >"${output_dir}/scan-result.json"
[codex] +
[codex] +if [[ -n "${GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY:-}" ]]; then
[codex] +  {
[codex] +    echo "## govulncheck"
[codex] +    echo
[codex] +    echo "Result: ${result}"
[codex] +    echo
[codex] +    echo "Text exit code: ${text_status}; JSON exit code: ${json_status}; JSON validation exit code: ${json_validation_status}."
[codex] +  } >>"${GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY}"
[codex] +fi
[codex] +
[codex] +if [[ "${result}" == "operational-error" ]]; then
[codex] +  echo "govulncheck failed to produce complete text and JSON evidence." >&2
[codex] +  exit 1
[codex] +fi
[codex] +
[codex] +if [[ "${result}" == "findings" ]]; then
[codex] +  echo "govulncheck reported reachable vulnerabilities; findings are recorded but are not a merge gate." >&2
[codex] +fi
[codex]

[codex] +

[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up analysis Go toolchain

[codex] +        continue-on-error: true

[codex] +          path: govulncheck-results/

[codex] +          retention-days: 7

[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +**Status:** Local configuration is prepared; automation is not active.
[codex] +
[codex] +The Budget Analyzer
[codex] +[dependency automation policy](https://github.com/budgetanalyzer/orchestration/blob/main/docs/dependency-automation.md)
[codex] +owns activation, scheduling, review, and cost boundaries. Renovate is the only
[codex] +update-PR owner. Dependabot remains alert-only, and neither routine nor security
[codex] +updates are automerged.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared orchestration preset. Native Renovate managers
[codex] +cover the Go directive and direct and indirect modules in `go.mod`, both pinned
[codex] +Dockerfile images, and GitHub Actions. A repository-specific regex manager covers
[codex] +the `GOVULNCHECK_VERSION` value in
[codex] +`.github/workflows/go-vulnerability-check.yml` as the Go module
[codex] +`golang.org/x/vuln`.
[codex] +
[codex] +The existing `build.yml` workflow remains the pull-request check for formatting,
[codex] +tests, and the service image build. Dependency automation does not bypass those
[codex] +checks. The shared preset must be published before this consumer configuration is
[codex] +activated. This onboarding does not update `go.mod`, `go.sum`, the Go directive,
[codex] +container images, Actions, or application code.
[codex] +
[codex] +A credential-free local run with Renovate 44.65.5 under Node 24 extracted 21
[codex] +occurrences from six files: two Dockerfile dependencies, 14 GitHub
[codex] +Actions/runner/Go inputs, four `go.mod` entries, and one regex-managed scanner
[codex] +pin. The module entries were `go`, `github.com/redis/go-redis/v9`,
[codex] +`github.com/cespare/xxhash/v2`, and `github.com/dgryski/go-rendezvous`. Renovate
[codex] +recognizes the latter two as indirect and leaves their standalone updates
[codex] +disabled; they remain represented in the module graph and can change through a
[codex] +reviewed direct-module update.
[codex] +
[codex] +The local lookup independently found go-redis 9.7.3 as the maintained-line patch
[codex] +and exposed later v9 release lines through 9.22.0. It also resolved the
[codex] +distroless runtime image and the current scanner module. The Go builder image was
[codex] +extracted, but Renovate's anonymous Docker Hub client returned `no-result` and an
[codex] +anonymous Docker-token warning. GitHub Actions and the workflow's Go 1.26.x input
[codex] +were extracted but skipped because no GitHub token was supplied. These incomplete
[codex] +lookups are retained for the hosted Phase 12 retry. Extraction completed without
[codex] +an exception or fatal log; its only error-level entry was the documented
[codex] +missing-GitHub-token condition.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Reachable vulnerability evidence
[codex] +
[codex] +`go-vulnerability-check.yml` runs after changes reach `main`, every Monday, and
[codex] +on manual dispatch. It installs the pinned official `govulncheck` CLI and runs
[codex] +both `govulncheck ./...` and `govulncheck -json ./...`. The workflow keeps the
[codex] +readable report, JSON event stream, stderr, scanner version, and exit-code summary
[codex] +for seven days.
[codex] +
[codex] +Reachable findings are reported and uploaded without failing unrelated work.
[codex] +Scanner installation, package loading, vulnerability database access, analysis,
[codex] +JSON generation or validation, empty required evidence, and artifact upload
[codex] +failures still fail the job. In particular, the text command's exit code 3 means
[codex] +findings, while any other unexpected text status or any nonzero JSON status is an
[codex] +operational error.
[codex] +
[codex] +The application still declares Go 1.24. The current pinned scanner,
[codex] +`golang.org/x/vuln` v1.7.0, requires Go 1.25 or newer, so this workflow uses Go
[codex] +1.26.x solely as its analysis toolchain and sets `GOTOOLCHAIN=local` to prevent an
[codex] +implicit toolchain change. That does not upgrade the application toolchain or its
[codex] +builder image.
[codex] +
[codex] +### Local baseline evidence
[codex] +
[codex] +On 2026-09-06, govulncheck v1.7.0 running with Go 1.26.8 reproduced
[codex] +GO-2025-3540 as a reachable vulnerability in
[codex] +`github.com/redis/go-redis/v9` 9.7.0. The fixed version reported by the scanner is
[codex] +9.7.3. A representative trace is:
[codex] +
[codex] +```text
[codex] +session.go:81:24: ext.NewSessionStore calls redis.cmdable.Ping,
[codex] +which eventually calls redis.baseClient.initConn
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +The scanner reported one reachable vulnerability from one module and no other
[codex] +vulnerabilities in imported packages or required modules. This call-path result
[codex] +is stronger evidence than module presence alone and reproduces the preserved
[codex] +September 6 review. No remediation upgrade is part of this onboarding phase.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the same local check with an explicit analysis toolchain and pinned scanner:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +scanner_bin="$(mktemp -d)"
[codex] +scan_output="$(mktemp -d)"
[codex] +GOBIN="${scanner_bin}" GOTOOLCHAIN=go1.26.8 \
[codex] +  go install golang.org/x/vuln/cmd/govulncheck@v1.7.0
[codex] +PATH="${scanner_bin}:${PATH}" GOTOOLCHAIN=go1.26.8 \
[codex] +  ./scripts/run-govulncheck.sh "${scan_output}"
[codex] +printf 'Evidence: %s\n' "${scan_output}"
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +The explicit local analysis toolchain may download that public Go release when
[codex] +it is not cached. It does not alter `go.mod` or the Dockerfile builder version.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 operator handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +The following evidence remains pending because this phase neither publishes
[codex] +configuration nor uses credentials:
[codex] +
[codex] +- Repository: `budgetanalyzer/ext-authz`. Check: a hosted Renovate full dry run
[codex] +  after the shared preset is published. Scope: `go.mod`, both Dockerfile images,
[codex] +  all workflow Actions and Go inputs, and the govulncheck pin. Reason: published
[codex] +  preset resolution and GitHub lookups require the Phase 12 operator context;
[codex] +  the local anonymous lookup also returned `Failed to look up docker package
[codex] +  golang: no-result`. Action: run the trusted read-only hosted validation and
[codex] +  then onboard the free Renovate App, retrying the Go builder lookup through the
[codex] +  hosted network. Proof: preserve the run URL and logs showing resolved policy,
[codex] +  extracted package identities, successful lookups including `golang:1.24-alpine`,
[codex] +  and no fatal/error output. If the hosted Docker lookup still fails, retain it
[codex] +  as a coverage gap instead of activating with an assumed result.
[codex] +- Repository: `budgetanalyzer/ext-authz`. Check: manually dispatch
[codex] +  `go-vulnerability-check.yml` from the trusted default branch. Scope:
[codex] +  `github.com/redis/go-redis/v9` 9.7.0, the Go module graph, scanner module, and
[codex] +  vulnerability database. Reason: the workflow is not published or activated
[codex] +  during local preparation. Action: dispatch it without exposing its ephemeral
[codex] +  GitHub token. Proof: preserve the run URL, source revision, and seven-day
[codex] +  artifact containing complete readable and JSON output; until remediation, it
[codex] +  should retain the GO-2025-3540 call path above.
[codex] +- Repository: `budgetanalyzer/ext-authz`. Check: GitHub dependency graph and
[codex] +  Dependabot alert status. Scope: the Go module graph and vulnerability alerts.
[codex] +  Reason: repository settings are administrator-owned. Action: enable the graph
[codex] +  and alerts, keep Dependabot version updates and overlapping security-update PRs
[codex] +  disabled, and grant Renovate alert-read access. Proof: sanitized settings and
[codex] +  alert evidence tied to this repository.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate discovery, Dependabot alerts, and govulncheck do not prove support
[codex] +lifecycle status. Go support remains part of the quarterly upstream lifecycle
[codex] +review, and the application Go 1.24 migration requires separate coordination.

[codex] +name: Go Vulnerability Check
[codex] +
[codex] +on:
[codex] +  push:
[codex] +    branches: [main]
[codex] +  schedule:
[codex] +    - cron: "17 5 * * 1"
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +
[codex] +permissions:
[codex] +  contents: read
[codex] +
[codex] +env:
[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: "true"
[codex] +  GOVULNCHECK_VERSION: "v1.7.0"
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  govulncheck:
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up analysis Go toolchain
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-go@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          go-version: "1.26.x"
[codex] +          cache: true
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Install govulncheck
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          scanner_bin="${RUNNER_TEMP}/govulncheck-bin"
[codex] +          mkdir -p "${scanner_bin}"
[codex] +          GOBIN="${scanner_bin}" GOTOOLCHAIN=local \
[codex] +            go install "golang.org/x/vuln/cmd/govulncheck@${GOVULNCHECK_VERSION}"
[codex] +          echo "${scanner_bin}" >> "${GITHUB_PATH}"
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Scan reachable Go vulnerabilities
[codex] +        id: scan
[codex] +        continue-on-error: true
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GOTOOLCHAIN: local
[codex] +        run: ./scripts/run-govulncheck.sh govulncheck-results
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Upload govulncheck evidence
[codex] +        if: always()
[codex] +        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v7
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          name: govulncheck-${{ github.sha }}
[codex] +          path: govulncheck-results/
[codex] +          if-no-files-found: error
[codex] +          retention-days: 7
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Enforce scanner completion
[codex] +        if: always()
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          SCAN_OUTCOME: ${{ steps.scan.outcome }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ "${SCAN_OUTCOME}" != "success" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "govulncheck did not complete successfully; inspect the uploaded evidence." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi

[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +**Status:** Local configuration is prepared; automation is not active.
[codex] +
[codex] +The Budget Analyzer
[codex] +[dependency automation policy](https://github.com/budgetanalyzer/orchestration/blob/main/docs/dependency-automation.md)
[codex] +owns activation, scheduling, review, and cost boundaries. Renovate is the only
[codex] +update-PR owner. Dependabot remains alert-only, and neither routine nor security
[codex] +updates are automerged.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared orchestration preset. Native Renovate managers
[codex] +cover the Go directive and direct and indirect modules in `go.mod`, both pinned
[codex] +Dockerfile images, and GitHub Actions. A repository-specific regex manager covers
[codex] +the `GOVULNCHECK_VERSION` value in
[codex] +`.github/workflows/go-vulnerability-check.yml` as the Go module
[codex] +`golang.org/x/vuln`.
[codex] +
[codex] +The existing `build.yml` workflow remains the pull-request check for formatting,
[codex] +tests, and the service image build. Dependency automation does not bypass those
[codex] +checks. The shared preset must be published before this consumer configuration is
[codex] +activated. This onboarding does not update `go.mod`, `go.sum`, the Go directive,
[codex] +container images, Actions, or application code.
[codex] +
[codex] +A credential-free local run with Renovate 44.65.5 under Node 24 extracted 21
[codex] +occurrences from six files: two Dockerfile dependencies, 14 GitHub
[codex] +Actions/runner/Go inputs, four `go.mod` entries, and one regex-managed scanner
[codex] +pin. The module entries were `go`, `github.com/redis/go-redis/v9`,
[codex] +`github.com/cespare/xxhash/v2`, and `github.com/dgryski/go-rendezvous`. Renovate
[codex] +recognizes the latter two as indirect and leaves their standalone updates
[codex] +disabled; they remain represented in the module graph and can change through a
[codex] +reviewed direct-module update.
[codex] +
[codex] +The local lookup independently found go-redis 9.7.3 as the maintained-line patch
[codex] +and exposed later v9 release lines through 9.22.0. It also resolved the
[codex] +distroless runtime image and the current scanner module. The Go builder image was
[codex] +extracted, but Renovate's anonymous Docker Hub client returned `no-result` and an
[codex] +anonymous Docker-token warning. GitHub Actions and the workflow's Go 1.26.x input
[codex] +were extracted but skipped because no GitHub token was supplied. These incomplete
[codex] +lookups are retained for the hosted Phase 12 retry. Extraction completed without
[codex] +an exception or fatal log; its only error-level entry was the documented
[codex] +missing-GitHub-token condition.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Reachable vulnerability evidence
[codex] +
[codex] +`go-vulnerability-check.yml` runs after changes reach `main`, every Monday, and
[codex] +on manual dispatch. It installs the pinned official `govulncheck` CLI and runs
[codex] +both `govulncheck ./...` and `govulncheck -json ./...`. The workflow keeps the
[codex] +readable report, JSON event stream, stderr, scanner version, and exit-code summary
[codex] +for seven days.
[codex] +
[codex] +Reachable findings are reported and uploaded without failing unrelated work.
[codex] +Scanner installation, package loading, vulnerability database access, analysis,
[codex] +JSON generation or validation, empty required evidence, and artifact upload
[codex] +failures still fail the job. In particular, the text command's exit code 3 means
[codex] +findings, while any other unexpected text status or any nonzero JSON status is an
[codex] +operational error.
[codex] +
[codex] +The application still declares Go 1.24. The current pinned scanner,
[codex] +`golang.org/x/vuln` v1.7.0, requires Go 1.25 or newer, so this workflow uses Go
[codex] +1.26.x solely as its analysis toolchain and sets `GOTOOLCHAIN=local` to prevent an
[codex] +implicit toolchain change. That does not upgrade the application toolchain or its
[codex] +builder image.
[codex] +
[codex] +### Local baseline evidence
[codex] +
[codex] +On 2026-09-06, govulncheck v1.7.0 running with Go 1.26.8 reproduced
[codex] +GO-2025-3540 as a reachable vulnerability in
[codex] +`github.com/redis/go-redis/v9` 9.7.0. The fixed version reported by the scanner is
[codex] +9.7.3. A representative trace is:
[codex] +
[codex] +```text
[codex] +session.go:81:24: ext.NewSessionStore calls redis.cmdable.Ping,
[codex] +which eventually calls redis.baseClient.initConn
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +The scanner reported one reachable vulnerability from one module and no other
[codex] +vulnerabilities in imported packages or required modules. This call-path result
[codex] +is stronger evidence than module presence alone and reproduces the preserved
[codex] +September 6 review. No remediation upgrade is part of this onboarding phase.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the same local check with an explicit analysis toolchain and pinned scanner:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +scanner_bin="$(mktemp -d)"
[codex] +scan_output="$(mktemp -d)"
[codex] +GOBIN="${scanner_bin}" GOTOOLCHAIN=go1.26.8 \
[codex] +  go install golang.org/x/vuln/cmd/govulncheck@v1.7.0
[codex] +PATH="${scanner_bin}:${PATH}" GOTOOLCHAIN=go1.26.8 \
[codex] +  ./scripts/run-govulncheck.sh "${scan_output}"
[codex] +printf 'Evidence: %s\n' "${scan_output}"
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +The explicit local analysis toolchain may download that public Go release when
[codex] +it is not cached. It does not alter `go.mod` or the Dockerfile builder version.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 operator handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +The following evidence remains pending because this phase neither publishes
[codex] +configuration nor uses credentials:
[codex] +
[codex] +- Repository: `budgetanalyzer/ext-authz`. Check: a hosted Renovate full dry run
[codex] +  after the shared preset is published. Scope: `go.mod`, both Dockerfile images,
[codex] +  all workflow Actions and Go inputs, and the govulncheck pin. Reason: published
[codex] +  preset resolution and GitHub lookups require the Phase 12 operator context;
[codex] +  the local anonymous lookup also returned `Failed to look up docker package
[codex] +  golang: no-result`. Action: run the trusted read-only hosted validation and
[codex] +  then onboard the free Renovate App, retrying the Go builder lookup through the
[codex] +  hosted network. Proof: preserve the run URL and logs showing resolved policy,
[codex] +  extracted package identities, successful lookups including `golang:1.24-alpine`,
[codex] +  and no fatal/error output. If the hosted Docker lookup still fails, retain it
[codex] +  as a coverage gap instead of activating with an assumed result.
[codex] +- Repository: `budgetanalyzer/ext-authz`. Check: manually dispatch
[codex] +  `go-vulnerability-check.yml` from the trusted default branch. Scope:
[codex] +  `github.com/redis/go-redis/v9` 9.7.0, the Go module graph, scanner module, and
[codex] +  vulnerability database. Reason: the workflow is not published or activated
[codex] +  during local preparation. Action: dispatch it without exposing its ephemeral
[codex] +  GitHub token. Proof: preserve the run URL, source revision, and seven-day
[codex] +  artifact containing complete readable and JSON output; until remediation, it
[codex] +  should retain the GO-2025-3540 call path above.
[codex] +- Repository: `budgetanalyzer/ext-authz`. Check: GitHub dependency graph and
[codex] +  Dependabot alert status. Scope: the Go module graph and vulnerability alerts.
[codex] +  Reason: repository settings are administrator-owned. Action: enable the graph
[codex] +  and alerts, keep Dependabot version updates and overlapping security-update PRs
[codex] +  disabled, and grant Renovate alert-read access. Proof: sanitized settings and
[codex] +  alert evidence tied to this repository.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate discovery, Dependabot alerts, and govulncheck do not prove support
[codex] +lifecycle status. Go support remains part of the quarterly upstream lifecycle
[codex] +review, and the application Go 1.24 migration requires separate coordination.

[codex] +++ b/scripts/run-govulncheck.sh
[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,76 @@
[codex] +#!/usr/bin/env bash
[codex] +
[codex] +set -uo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +output_dir="${1:-govulncheck-results}"
[codex] +mkdir -p "${output_dir}"
[codex] +
[codex] +govulncheck -version >"${output_dir}/govulncheck-version.txt" 2>&1
[codex] +version_status=$?
[codex] +
[codex] +govulncheck ./... >"${output_dir}/govulncheck.txt" \
[codex] +  2>"${output_dir}/govulncheck-text.stderr"
[codex] +text_status=$?
[codex] +
[codex] +govulncheck -json ./... >"${output_dir}/govulncheck.json" \
[codex] +  2>"${output_dir}/govulncheck-json.stderr"
[codex] +json_status=$?
[codex] +
[codex] +json_validation_status=1
[codex] +if [[ -s "${output_dir}/govulncheck.json" ]]; then
[codex] +  jq -e . "${output_dir}/govulncheck.json" >/dev/null \
[codex] +    2>"${output_dir}/govulncheck-json-validation.stderr"
[codex] +  json_validation_status=$?
[codex] +else
[codex] +  echo "govulncheck JSON output is empty." \
[codex] +    >"${output_dir}/govulncheck-json-validation.stderr"
[codex] +fi
[codex] +
[codex] +evidence_status=0
[codex] +for evidence_file in govulncheck-version.txt govulncheck.txt; do
[codex] +  if [[ ! -s "${output_dir}/${evidence_file}" ]]; then
[codex] +    echo "Required evidence is empty: ${evidence_file}" >&2
[codex] +    evidence_status=1
[codex] +  fi
[codex] +done
[codex] +
[codex] +cat "${output_dir}/govulncheck.txt"
[codex] +cat "${output_dir}/govulncheck-text.stderr" >&2
[codex] +cat "${output_dir}/govulncheck-json.stderr" >&2
[codex] +
[codex] +result="operational-error"
[codex] +if [[ "${version_status}" -eq 0 && "${json_status}" -eq 0 && \
[codex] +  "${json_validation_status}" -eq 0 && "${evidence_status}" -eq 0 ]]; then
[codex] +  case "${text_status}" in
[codex] +    0)
[codex] +      result="clean"
[codex] +      ;;
[codex] +    3)
[codex] +      result="findings"
[codex] +      ;;
[codex] +  esac
[codex] +fi
[codex] +
[codex] +printf '{\n  "result": "%s",\n  "version_exit_code": %d,\n  "text_exit_code": %d,\n  "json_exit_code": %d,\n  "json_validation_exit_code": %d,\n  "evidence_exit_code": %d\n}\n' \
[codex] +  "${result}" "${version_status}" "${text_status}" "${json_status}" \
[codex] +  "${json_validation_status}" "${evidence_status}" \
[codex] +  >"${output_dir}/scan-result.json"
[codex] +
[codex] +if [[ -n "${GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY:-}" ]]; then
[codex] +  {
[codex] +    echo "## govulncheck"
[codex] +    echo
[codex] +    echo "Result: ${result}"
[codex] +    echo
[codex] +    echo "Text exit code: ${text_status}; JSON exit code: ${json_status}; JSON validation exit code: ${json_validation_status}."
[codex] +  } >>"${GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY}"
[codex] +fi
[codex] +
[codex] +if [[ "${result}" == "operational-error" ]]; then
[codex] +  echo "govulncheck failed to produce complete text and JSON evidence." >&2
[codex] +  exit 1
[codex] +fi
[codex] +
[codex] +if [[ "${result}" == "findings" ]]; then
[codex] +  echo "govulncheck reported reachable vulnerabilities; findings are recorded but are not a merge gate." >&2
[codex] +fi
[codex] 
[codex] exec
[codex] /bin/bash -lc 'failure_output="$(mktemp -d)"
[codex] govulncheck() { return 42; }
[codex] export -f govulncheck
[codex] set +e
[codex] ./scripts/run-govulncheck.sh "${failure_output}" >/dev/null 2>&1
[codex] failure_status=$?
[codex] set -e
[codex] unset -f govulncheck
[codex] printf '"'failure_status=%s\\n' \""'$failure_status"
[codex] jq . "${failure_output}/scan-result.json"
[codex] if [[ "$failure_status" -eq 0 ]]; then exit 1; fi' in /workspace/ext-authz
[codex]  succeeded in 0ms:
[codex] failure_status=1
[codex] {
[codex]   "result": "operational-error",
[codex]   "version_exit_code": 42,
[codex]   "text_exit_code": 42,
[codex]   "json_exit_code": 42,
[codex]   "json_validation_exit_code": 1,
[codex]   "evidence_exit_code": 1
[codex] }

[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +**Status:** Local configuration is prepared; automation is not active.
[codex] +
[codex] +The Budget Analyzer
[codex] +[dependency automation policy](https://github.com/budgetanalyzer/orchestration/blob/main/docs/dependency-automation.md)
[codex] +owns activation, scheduling, review, and cost boundaries. Renovate is the only
[codex] +update-PR owner. Dependabot remains alert-only, and neither routine nor security
[codex] +updates are automerged.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared orchestration preset. Native Renovate managers
[codex] +cover the Go directive and direct and indirect modules in `go.mod`, both pinned
[codex] +Dockerfile images, and GitHub Actions. A repository-specific regex manager covers
[codex] +the `GOVULNCHECK_VERSION` value in
[codex] +`.github/workflows/go-vulnerability-check.yml` as the Go module
[codex] +`golang.org/x/vuln`.
[codex] +
[codex] +The existing `build.yml` workflow remains the pull-request check for formatting,
[codex] +tests, and the service image build. Dependency automation does not bypass those
[codex] +checks. The shared preset must be published before this consumer configuration is
[codex] +activated. This onboarding does not update `go.mod`, `go.sum`, the Go directive,
[codex] +container images, Actions, or application code.
[codex] +
[codex] +A credential-free local run with Renovate 44.65.5 under Node 24 extracted 21
[codex] +occurrences from six files: two Dockerfile dependencies, 14 GitHub
[codex] +Actions/runner/Go inputs, four `go.mod` entries, and one regex-managed scanner
[codex] +pin. The module entries were `go`, `github.com/redis/go-redis/v9`,
[codex] +`github.com/cespare/xxhash/v2`, and `github.com/dgryski/go-rendezvous`. Renovate
[codex] +recognizes the latter two as indirect and leaves their standalone updates
[codex] +disabled; they remain represented in the module graph and can change through a
[codex] +reviewed direct-module update.
[codex] +
[codex] +The local lookup independently found go-redis 9.7.3 as the maintained-line patch
[codex] +and exposed later v9 release lines through 9.22.0. It also resolved the
[codex] +distroless runtime image and the current scanner module. The Go builder image was
[codex] +extracted, but Renovate's anonymous Docker Hub client returned `no-result` and an
[codex] +anonymous Docker-token warning. GitHub Actions and the workflow's Go 1.26.x input
[codex] +were extracted but skipped because no GitHub token was supplied. These incomplete
[codex] +lookups are retained for the hosted Phase 12 retry. Extraction completed without
[codex] +an exception or fatal log; its only error-level entry was the documented
[codex] +missing-GitHub-token condition.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Reachable vulnerability evidence
[codex] +
[codex] +`go-vulnerability-check.yml` runs after changes reach `main`, every Monday, and
[codex] +on manual dispatch. It installs the pinned official `govulncheck` CLI and runs
[codex] +both `govulncheck ./...` and `govulncheck -json ./...`. The workflow keeps the
[codex] +readable report, JSON event stream, stderr, scanner version, and exit-code summary
[codex] +for seven days.
[codex] +
[codex] +Reachable findings are reported and uploaded without failing unrelated work.
[codex] +Scanner installation, package loading, vulnerability database access, analysis,
[codex] +JSON generation or validation, empty required evidence, and artifact upload
[codex] +failures still fail the job. In particular, the text command's exit code 3 means
[codex] +findings, while any other unexpected text status or any nonzero JSON status is an
[codex] +operational error.
[codex] +
[codex] +The application still declares Go 1.24. The current pinned scanner,
[codex] +`golang.org/x/vuln` v1.7.0, requires Go 1.25 or newer, so this workflow uses Go
[codex] +1.26.x solely as its analysis toolchain and sets `GOTOOLCHAIN=local` to prevent an
[codex] +implicit toolchain change. That does not upgrade the application toolchain or its
[codex] +builder image.
[codex] +
[codex] +### Local baseline evidence
[codex] +
[codex] +On 2026-09-06, govulncheck v1.7.0 running with Go 1.26.8 reproduced
[codex] +GO-2025-3540 as a reachable vulnerability in
[codex] +`github.com/redis/go-redis/v9` 9.7.0. The fixed version reported by the scanner is
[codex] +9.7.3. A representative trace is:
[codex] +
[codex] +```text
[codex] +session.go:81:24: ext.NewSessionStore calls redis.cmdable.Ping,
[codex] +which eventually calls redis.baseClient.initConn
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +The scanner reported one reachable vulnerability from one module and no other
[codex] +vulnerabilities in imported packages or required modules. This call-path result
[codex] +is stronger evidence than module presence alone and reproduces the preserved
[codex] +September 6 review. No remediation upgrade is part of this onboarding phase.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the same local check with an explicit analysis toolchain and pinned scanner:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +scanner_bin="$(mktemp -d)"
[codex] +scan_output="$(mktemp -d)"
[codex] +GOBIN="${scanner_bin}" GOTOOLCHAIN=go1.26.8 \
[codex] +  go install golang.org/x/vuln/cmd/govulncheck@v1.7.0
[codex] +PATH="${scanner_bin}:${PATH}" GOTOOLCHAIN=go1.26.8 \
[codex] +  ./scripts/run-govulncheck.sh "${scan_output}"
[codex] +printf 'Evidence: %s\n' "${scan_output}"
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +The explicit local analysis toolchain may download that public Go release when
[codex] +it is not cached. It does not alter `go.mod` or the Dockerfile builder version.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 operator handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +The following evidence remains pending because this phase neither publishes
[codex] +configuration nor uses credentials:
[codex] +
[codex] +- Repository: `budgetanalyzer/ext-authz`. Check: a hosted Renovate full dry run
[codex] +  after the shared preset is published. Scope: `go.mod`, both Dockerfile images,
[codex] +  all workflow Actions and Go inputs, and the govulncheck pin. Reason: published
[codex] +  preset resolution and GitHub lookups require the Phase 12 operator context;
[codex] +  the local anonymous lookup also returned `Failed to look up docker package
[codex] +  golang: no-result`. Action: run the trusted read-only hosted validation and
[codex] +  then onboard the free Renovate App, retrying the Go builder lookup through the
[codex] +  hosted network. Proof: preserve the run URL and logs showing resolved policy,
[codex] +  extracted package identities, successful lookups including `golang:1.24-alpine`,
[codex] +  and no fatal/error output. If the hosted Docker lookup still fails, retain it
[codex] +  as a coverage gap instead of activating with an assumed result.
[codex] +- Repository: `budgetanalyzer/ext-authz`. Check: manually dispatch
[codex] +  `go-vulnerability-check.yml` from the trusted default branch. Scope:
[codex] +  `github.com/redis/go-redis/v9` 9.7.0, the Go module graph, scanner module, and
[codex] +  vulnerability database. Reason: the workflow is not published or activated
[codex] +  during local preparation. Action: dispatch it without exposing its ephemeral
[codex] +  GitHub token. Proof: preserve the run URL, source revision, and seven-day
[codex] +  artifact containing complete readable and JSON output; until remediation, it
[codex] +  should retain the GO-2025-3540 call path above.
[codex] +- Repository: `budgetanalyzer/ext-authz`. Check: GitHub dependency graph and
[codex] +  Dependabot alert status. Scope: the Go module graph and vulnerability alerts.
[codex] +  Reason: repository settings are administrator-owned. Action: enable the graph
[codex] +  and alerts, keep Dependabot version updates and overlapping security-update PRs
[codex] +  disabled, and grant Renovate alert-read access. Proof: sanitized settings and
[codex] +  alert evidence tied to this repository.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate discovery, Dependabot alerts, and govulncheck do not prove support
[codex] +lifecycle status. Go support remains part of the quarterly upstream lifecycle
[codex] +review, and the application Go 1.24 migration requires separate coordination.

[codex] index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..6fdaa1678005e71f802be4a317bac49dbaf75c7e

[codex] +++ b/scripts/run-govulncheck.sh
[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,76 @@
[codex] +#!/usr/bin/env bash
[codex] +
[codex] +set -uo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +output_dir="${1:-govulncheck-results}"
[codex] +mkdir -p "${output_dir}"
[codex] +
[codex] +govulncheck -version >"${output_dir}/govulncheck-version.txt" 2>&1
[codex] +version_status=$?
[codex] +
[codex] +govulncheck ./... >"${output_dir}/govulncheck.txt" \
[codex] +  2>"${output_dir}/govulncheck-text.stderr"
[codex] +text_status=$?
[codex] +
[codex] +govulncheck -json ./... >"${output_dir}/govulncheck.json" \
[codex] +  2>"${output_dir}/govulncheck-json.stderr"
[codex] +json_status=$?
[codex] +
[codex] +json_validation_status=1
[codex] +if [[ -s "${output_dir}/govulncheck.json" ]]; then
[codex] +  jq -e . "${output_dir}/govulncheck.json" >/dev/null \
[codex] +    2>"${output_dir}/govulncheck-json-validation.stderr"
[codex] +  json_validation_status=$?
[codex] +else
[codex] +  echo "govulncheck JSON output is empty." \
[codex] +    >"${output_dir}/govulncheck-json-validation.stderr"
[codex] +fi
[codex] +
[codex] +evidence_status=0
[codex] +for evidence_file in govulncheck-version.txt govulncheck.txt; do
[codex] +  if [[ ! -s "${output_dir}/${evidence_file}" ]]; then
[codex] +    echo "Required evidence is empty: ${evidence_file}" >&2
[codex] +    evidence_status=1
[codex] +  fi
[codex] +done
[codex] +
[codex] +cat "${output_dir}/govulncheck.txt"
[codex] +cat "${output_dir}/govulncheck-text.stderr" >&2
[codex] +cat "${output_dir}/govulncheck-json.stderr" >&2
[codex] +
[codex] +result="operational-error"
[codex] +if [[ "${version_status}" -eq 0 && "${json_status}" -eq 0 && \
[codex] +  "${json_validation_status}" -eq 0 && "${evidence_status}" -eq 0 ]]; then
[codex] +  case "${text_status}" in
[codex] +    0)
[codex] +      result="clean"
[codex] +      ;;
[codex] +    3)
[codex] +      result="findings"
[codex] +      ;;
[codex] +  esac
[codex] +fi
[codex] +
[codex] +printf '{\n  "result": "%s",\n  "version_exit_code": %d,\n  "text_exit_code": %d,\n  "json_exit_code": %d,\n  "json_validation_exit_code": %d,\n  "evidence_exit_code": %d\n}\n' \
[codex] +  "${result}" "${version_status}" "${text_status}" "${json_status}" \
[codex] +  "${json_validation_status}" "${evidence_status}" \
[codex] +  >"${output_dir}/scan-result.json"
[codex] +
[codex] +if [[ -n "${GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY:-}" ]]; then
[codex] +  {
[codex] +    echo "## govulncheck"
[codex] +    echo
[codex] +    echo "Result: ${result}"
[codex] +    echo
[codex] +    echo "Text exit code: ${text_status}; JSON exit code: ${json_status}; JSON validation exit code: ${json_validation_status}."
[codex] +  } >>"${GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY}"
[codex] +fi
[codex] +
[codex] +if [[ "${result}" == "operational-error" ]]; then
[codex] +  echo "govulncheck failed to produce complete text and JSON evidence." >&2
[codex] +  exit 1
[codex] +fi
[codex] +
[codex] +if [[ "${result}" == "findings" ]]; then
[codex] +  echo "govulncheck reported reachable vulnerabilities; findings are recorded but are not a merge gate." >&2
[codex] +fi
[codex] 
[codex] exec
[codex] /bin/bash -lc "/home/vscode/.npm/_npx/e11c424cfc990a60/node_modules/.bin/renovate-config-validator --strict --no-global renovate.json
[codex] actionlint .github/workflows/*.yml

[codex] +name: Go Vulnerability Check
[codex] +
[codex] +on:
[codex] +  push:
[codex] +    branches: [main]
[codex] +  schedule:
[codex] +    - cron: "17 5 * * 1"
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +
[codex] +permissions:
[codex] +  contents: read
[codex] +
[codex] +env:
[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: "true"
[codex] +  GOVULNCHECK_VERSION: "v1.7.0"
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  govulncheck:
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up analysis Go toolchain
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-go@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          go-version: "1.26.x"
[codex] +          cache: true
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Install govulncheck
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          scanner_bin="${RUNNER_TEMP}/govulncheck-bin"
[codex] +          mkdir -p "${scanner_bin}"
[codex] +          GOBIN="${scanner_bin}" GOTOOLCHAIN=local \
[codex] +            go install "golang.org/x/vuln/cmd/govulncheck@${GOVULNCHECK_VERSION}"
[codex] +          echo "${scanner_bin}" >> "${GITHUB_PATH}"
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Scan reachable Go vulnerabilities
[codex] +        id: scan
[codex] +        continue-on-error: true
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GOTOOLCHAIN: local
[codex] +        run: ./scripts/run-govulncheck.sh govulncheck-results
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Upload govulncheck evidence
[codex] +        if: always()
[codex] +        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v7
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          name: govulncheck-${{ github.sha }}
[codex] +          path: govulncheck-results/
[codex] +          if-no-files-found: error
[codex] +          retention-days: 7
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Enforce scanner completion
[codex] +        if: always()
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          SCAN_OUTCOME: ${{ steps.scan.outcome }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ "${SCAN_OUTCOME}" != "success" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "govulncheck did not complete successfully; inspect the uploaded evidence." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi

[codex] +
[codex] +The application still declares Go 1.24. The current pinned scanner,
[codex] +`golang.org/x/vuln` v1.7.0, requires Go 1.25 or newer, so this workflow uses Go
[codex] +1.26.x solely as its analysis toolchain and sets `GOTOOLCHAIN=local` to prevent an
[codex] +implicit toolchain change. That does not upgrade the application toolchain or its
[codex] +builder image.
[codex] +
[codex] +### Local baseline evidence
[codex] +
[codex] +On 2026-09-06, govulncheck v1.7.0 running with Go 1.26.8 reproduced
[codex] +GO-2025-3540 as a reachable vulnerability in
[codex] +`github.com/redis/go-redis/v9` 9.7.0. The fixed version reported by the scanner is
[codex] +9.7.3. A representative trace is:
[codex] +
[codex] +```text
[codex] +session.go:81:24: ext.NewSessionStore calls redis.cmdable.Ping,
[codex] +which eventually calls redis.baseClient.initConn
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +The scanner reported one reachable vulnerability from one module and no other
[codex] +vulnerabilities in imported packages or required modules. This call-path result
[codex] +is stronger evidence than module presence alone and reproduces the preserved
[codex] +September 6 review. No remediation upgrade is part of this onboarding phase.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the same local check with an explicit analysis toolchain and pinned scanner:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +scanner_bin="$(mktemp -d)"
[codex] +scan_output="$(mktemp -d)"
[codex] +GOBIN="${scanner_bin}" GOTOOLCHAIN=go1.26.8 \
[codex] +  go install golang.org/x/vuln/cmd/govulncheck@v1.7.0
[codex] +PATH="${scanner_bin}:${PATH}" GOTOOLCHAIN=go1.26.8 \
[codex] +  ./scripts/run-govulncheck.sh "${scan_output}"
[codex] +printf 'Evidence: %s\n' "${scan_output}"
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +The explicit local analysis toolchain may download that public Go release when
[codex] +it is not cached. It does not alter `go.mod` or the Dockerfile builder version.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 operator handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +The following evidence remains pending because this phase neither publishes
[codex] +configuration nor uses credentials:
[codex] +
[codex] +- Repository: `budgetanalyzer/ext-authz`. Check: a hosted Renovate full dry run
[codex] +  after the shared preset is published. Scope: `go.mod`, both Dockerfile images,
[codex] +  all workflow Actions and Go inputs, and the govulncheck pin. Reason: published
[codex] +  preset resolution and GitHub lookups require the Phase 12 operator context;
[codex] +  the local anonymous lookup also returned `Failed to look up docker package
[codex] +  golang: no-result`. Action: run the trusted read-only hosted validation and
[codex] +  then onboard the free Renovate App, retrying the Go builder lookup through the
[codex] +  hosted network. Proof: preserve the run URL and logs showing resolved policy,
[codex] +  extracted package identities, successful lookups including `golang:1.24-alpine`,
[codex] +  and no fatal/error output. If the hosted Docker lookup still fails, retain it
[codex] +  as a coverage gap instead of activating with an assumed result.
[codex] +- Repository: `budgetanalyzer/ext-authz`. Check: manually dispatch
[codex] +  `go-vulnerability-check.yml` from the trusted default branch. Scope:
[codex] +  `github.com/redis/go-redis/v9` 9.7.0, the Go module graph, scanner module, and
[codex] +  vulnerability database. Reason: the workflow is not published or activated
[codex] +  during local preparation. Action: dispatch it without exposing its ephemeral
[codex] +  GitHub token. Proof: preserve the run URL, source revision, and seven-day
[codex] +  artifact containing complete readable and JSON output; until remediation, it
[codex] +  should retain the GO-2025-3540 call path above.
[codex] +- Repository: `budgetanalyzer/ext-authz`. Check: GitHub dependency graph and
[codex] +  Dependabot alert status. Scope: the Go module graph and vulnerability alerts.
[codex] +  Reason: repository settings are administrator-owned. Action: enable the graph
[codex] +  and alerts, keep Dependabot version updates and overlapping security-update PRs
[codex] +  disabled, and grant Renovate alert-read access. Proof: sanitized settings and
[codex] +  alert evidence tied to this repository.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate discovery, Dependabot alerts, and govulncheck do not prove support
[codex] +lifecycle status. Go support remains part of the quarterly upstream lifecycle
[codex] +review, and the application Go 1.24 migration requires separate coordination.

[codex] index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..6fdaa1678005e71f802be4a317bac49dbaf75c7e
[codex] --- /dev/null
[codex] +++ b/scripts/run-govulncheck.sh
[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,76 @@
[codex] +#!/usr/bin/env bash
[codex] +
[codex] +set -uo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +output_dir="${1:-govulncheck-results}"
[codex] +mkdir -p "${output_dir}"
[codex] +
[codex] +govulncheck -version >"${output_dir}/govulncheck-version.txt" 2>&1
[codex] +version_status=$?
[codex] +
[codex] +govulncheck ./... >"${output_dir}/govulncheck.txt" \
[codex] +  2>"${output_dir}/govulncheck-text.stderr"
[codex] +text_status=$?
[codex] +
[codex] +govulncheck -json ./... >"${output_dir}/govulncheck.json" \
[codex] +  2>"${output_dir}/govulncheck-json.stderr"
[codex] +json_status=$?
[codex] +
[codex] +json_validation_status=1
[codex] +if [[ -s "${output_dir}/govulncheck.json" ]]; then
[codex] +  jq -e . "${output_dir}/govulncheck.json" >/dev/null \
[codex] +    2>"${output_dir}/govulncheck-json-validation.stderr"
[codex] +  json_validation_status=$?
[codex] +else
[codex] +  echo "govulncheck JSON output is empty." \
[codex] +    >"${output_dir}/govulncheck-json-validation.stderr"
[codex] +fi
[codex] +
[codex] +evidence_status=0
[codex] +for evidence_file in govulncheck-version.txt govulncheck.txt; do
[codex] +  if [[ ! -s "${output_dir}/${evidence_file}" ]]; then
[codex] +    echo "Required evidence is empty: ${evidence_file}" >&2
[codex] +    evidence_status=1
[codex] +  fi
[codex] +done
[codex] +
[codex] +cat "${output_dir}/govulncheck.txt"
[codex] +cat "${output_dir}/govulncheck-text.stderr" >&2
[codex] +cat "${output_dir}/govulncheck-json.stderr" >&2
[codex] +
[codex] +result="operational-error"
[codex] +if [[ "${version_status}" -eq 0 && "${json_status}" -eq 0 && \
[codex] +  "${json_validation_status}" -eq 0 && "${evidence_status}" -eq 0 ]]; then
[codex] +  case "${text_status}" in
[codex] +    0)
[codex] +      result="clean"
[codex] +      ;;
[codex] +    3)
[codex] +      result="findings"
[codex] +      ;;
[codex] +  esac
[codex] +fi
[codex] +
[codex] +printf '{\n  "result": "%s",\n  "version_exit_code": %d,\n  "text_exit_code": %d,\n  "json_exit_code": %d,\n  "json_validation_exit_code": %d,\n  "evidence_exit_code": %d\n}\n' \
[codex] +  "${result}" "${version_status}" "${text_status}" "${json_status}" \
[codex] +  "${json_validation_status}" "${evidence_status}" \
[codex] +  >"${output_dir}/scan-result.json"
[codex] +
[codex] +if [[ -n "${GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY:-}" ]]; then
[codex] +  {
[codex] +    echo "## govulncheck"
[codex] +    echo
[codex] +    echo "Result: ${result}"
[codex] +    echo
[codex] +    echo "Text exit code: ${text_status}; JSON exit code: ${json_status}; JSON validation exit code: ${json_validation_status}."
[codex] +  } >>"${GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY}"
[codex] +fi
[codex] +
[codex] +if [[ "${result}" == "operational-error" ]]; then
[codex] +  echo "govulncheck failed to produce complete text and JSON evidence." >&2
[codex] +  exit 1
[codex] +fi
[codex] +
[codex] +if [[ "${result}" == "findings" ]]; then
[codex] +  echo "govulncheck reported reachable vulnerabilities; findings are recorded but are not a merge gate." >&2
[codex] +fi
[codex]

[codex] +

[codex] +  contents: read
[codex] +

[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  govulncheck:
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up analysis Go toolchain
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-go@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          go-version: "1.26.x"
[codex] +          cache: true
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Install govulncheck
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          scanner_bin="${RUNNER_TEMP}/govulncheck-bin"
[codex] +          mkdir -p "${scanner_bin}"
[codex] +          GOBIN="${scanner_bin}" GOTOOLCHAIN=local \
[codex] +            go install "golang.org/x/vuln/cmd/govulncheck@${GOVULNCHECK_VERSION}"
[codex] +          echo "${scanner_bin}" >> "${GITHUB_PATH}"
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Scan reachable Go vulnerabilities
[codex] +        id: scan
[codex] +        continue-on-error: true
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GOTOOLCHAIN: local
[codex] +        run: ./scripts/run-govulncheck.sh govulncheck-results
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Upload govulncheck evidence
[codex] +        if: always()
[codex] +        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v7
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          name: govulncheck-${{ github.sha }}
[codex] +          path: govulncheck-results/
[codex] +          if-no-files-found: error
[codex] +          retention-days: 7
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Enforce scanner completion
[codex] +        if: always()
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          SCAN_OUTCOME: ${{ steps.scan.outcome }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ "${SCAN_OUTCOME}" != "success" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "govulncheck did not complete successfully; inspect the uploaded evidence." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi

[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +**Status:** Local configuration is prepared; automation is not active.
[codex] +
[codex] +The Budget Analyzer
[codex] +[dependency automation policy](https://github.com/budgetanalyzer/orchestration/blob/main/docs/dependency-automation.md)
[codex] +owns activation, scheduling, review, and cost boundaries. Renovate is the only
[codex] +update-PR owner. Dependabot remains alert-only, and neither routine nor security
[codex] +updates are automerged.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared orchestration preset. Native Renovate managers
[codex] +cover the Go directive and direct and indirect modules in `go.mod`, both pinned
[codex] +Dockerfile images, and GitHub Actions. A repository-specific regex manager covers
[codex] +the `GOVULNCHECK_VERSION` value in
[codex] +`.github/workflows/go-vulnerability-check.yml` as the Go module
[codex] +`golang.org/x/vuln`.
[codex] +
[codex] +The existing `build.yml` workflow remains the pull-request check for formatting,
[codex] +tests, and the service image build. Dependency automation does not bypass those
[codex] +checks. The shared preset must be published before this consumer configuration is
[codex] +activated. This onboarding does not update `go.mod`, `go.sum`, the Go directive,
[codex] +container images, Actions, or application code.
[codex] +
[codex] +A credential-free local run with Renovate 44.65.5 under Node 24 extracted 21
[codex] +occurrences from six files: two Dockerfile dependencies, 14 GitHub
[codex] +Actions/runner/Go inputs, four `go.mod` entries, and one regex-managed scanner
[codex] +pin. The module entries were `go`, `github.com/redis/go-redis/v9`,
[codex] +`github.com/cespare/xxhash/v2`, and `github.com/dgryski/go-rendezvous`. Renovate
[codex] +recognizes the latter two as indirect and leaves their standalone updates
[codex] +disabled; they remain represented in the module graph and can change through a
[codex] +reviewed direct-module update.
[codex] +
[codex] +The local lookup independently found go-redis 9.7.3 as the maintained-line patch
[codex] +and exposed later v9 release lines through 9.22.0. It also resolved the
[codex] +distroless runtime image and the current scanner module. The Go builder image was
[codex] +extracted, but Renovate's anonymous Docker Hub client returned `no-result` and an
[codex] +anonymous Docker-token warning. GitHub Actions and the workflow's Go 1.26.x input
[codex] +were extracted but skipped because no GitHub token was supplied. These incomplete
[codex] +lookups are retained for the hosted Phase 12 retry. Extraction completed without
[codex] +an exception or fatal log; its only error-level entry was the documented
[codex] +missing-GitHub-token condition.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Reachable vulnerability evidence
[codex] +
[codex] +`go-vulnerability-check.yml` runs after changes reach `main`, every Monday, and
[codex] +on manual dispatch. It installs the pinned official `govulncheck` CLI and runs
[codex] +both `govulncheck ./...` and `govulncheck -json ./...`. The workflow keeps the
[codex] +readable report, JSON event stream, stderr, scanner version, and exit-code summary
[codex] +for seven days.
[codex] +
[codex] +Reachable findings are reported and uploaded without failing unrelated work.
[codex] +Scanner installation, package loading, vulnerability database access, analysis,
[codex] +JSON generation or validation, empty required evidence, and artifact upload
[codex] +failures still fail the job. In particular, the text command's exit code 3 means
[codex] +findings, while any other unexpected text status or any nonzero JSON status is an
[codex] +operational error.
[codex] +
[codex] +The application still declares Go 1.24. The current pinned scanner,
[codex] +`golang.org/x/vuln` v1.7.0, requires Go 1.25 or newer, so this workflow uses Go
[codex] +1.26.x solely as its analysis toolchain and sets `GOTOOLCHAIN=local` to prevent an
[codex] +implicit toolchain change. That does not upgrade the application toolchain or its
[codex] +builder image.
[codex] +
[codex] +### Local baseline evidence
[codex] +
[codex] +On 2026-09-06, govulncheck v1.7.0 running with Go 1.26.8 reproduced
[codex] +GO-2025-3540 as a reachable vulnerability in
[codex] +`github.com/redis/go-redis/v9` 9.7.0. The fixed version reported by the scanner is
[codex] +9.7.3. A representative trace is:
[codex] +
[codex] +```text
[codex] +session.go:81:24: ext.NewSessionStore calls redis.cmdable.Ping,
[codex] +which eventually calls redis.baseClient.initConn
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +The scanner reported one reachable vulnerability from one module and no other
[codex] +vulnerabilities in imported packages or required modules. This call-path result
[codex] +is stronger evidence than module presence alone and reproduces the preserved
[codex] +September 6 review. No remediation upgrade is part of this onboarding phase.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the same local check with an explicit analysis toolchain and pinned scanner:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash

[codex] +
[codex] +output_dir="${1:-govulncheck-results}"
[codex] +mkdir -p "${output_dir}"
[codex] +
[codex] +govulncheck -version >"${output_dir}/govulncheck-version.txt" 2>&1
[codex] +version_status=$?
[codex] +
[codex] +govulncheck ./... >"${output_dir}/govulncheck.txt" \
[codex] +  2>"${output_dir}/govulncheck-text.stderr"
[codex] +text_status=$?
[codex] +
[codex] +govulncheck -json ./... >"${output_dir}/govulncheck.json" \
[codex] +  2>"${output_dir}/govulncheck-json.stderr"
[codex] +json_status=$?
[codex] +
[codex] +json_validation_status=1
[codex] +if [[ -s "${output_dir}/govulncheck.json" ]]; then
[codex] +  jq -e . "${output_dir}/govulncheck.json" >/dev/null \
[codex] +    2>"${output_dir}/govulncheck-json-validation.stderr"
[codex] +  json_validation_status=$?
[codex] +else
[codex] +  echo "govulncheck JSON output is empty." \
[codex] +    >"${output_dir}/govulncheck-json-validation.stderr"
[codex] +fi
[codex] +
[codex] +evidence_status=0
[codex] +for evidence_file in govulncheck-version.txt govulncheck.txt; do
[codex] +  if [[ ! -s "${output_dir}/${evidence_file}" ]]; then
[codex] +    echo "Required evidence is empty: ${evidence_file}" >&2
[codex] +    evidence_status=1
[codex] +  fi
[codex] +done
[codex] +
[codex] +cat "${output_dir}/govulncheck.txt"
[codex] +cat "${output_dir}/govulncheck-text.stderr" >&2
[codex] +cat "${output_dir}/govulncheck-json.stderr" >&2
[codex] +
[codex] +result="operational-error"
[codex] +if [[ "${version_status}" -eq 0 && "${json_status}" -eq 0 && \
[codex] +  "${json_validation_status}" -eq 0 && "${evidence_status}" -eq 0 ]]; then
[codex] +  case "${text_status}" in
[codex] +    0)
[codex] +      result="clean"
[codex] +      ;;
[codex] +    3)
[codex] +      result="findings"
[codex] +      ;;
[codex] +  esac
[codex] +fi
[codex] +
[codex] +printf '{\n  "result": "%s",\n  "version_exit_code": %d,\n  "text_exit_code": %d,\n  "json_exit_code": %d,\n  "json_validation_exit_code": %d,\n  "evidence_exit_code": %d\n}\n' \
[codex] +  "${result}" "${version_status}" "${text_status}" "${json_status}" \
[codex] +  "${json_validation_status}" "${evidence_status}" \
[codex] +  >"${output_dir}/scan-result.json"
[codex] +
[codex] +if [[ -n "${GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY:-}" ]]; then
[codex] +  {
[codex] +    echo "## govulncheck"
[codex] +    echo
[codex] +    echo "Result: ${result}"
[codex] +    echo
[codex] +    echo "Text exit code: ${text_status}; JSON exit code: ${json_status}; JSON validation exit code: ${json_validation_status}."
[codex] +  } >>"${GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY}"
[codex] +fi
[codex] +
[codex] +if [[ "${result}" == "operational-error" ]]; then
[codex] +  echo "govulncheck failed to produce complete text and JSON evidence." >&2
[codex] +  exit 1
[codex] +fi
[codex] +
[codex] +if [[ "${result}" == "findings" ]]; then
[codex] +  echo "govulncheck reported reachable vulnerabilities; findings are recorded but are not a merge gate." >&2
[codex] +fi
[codex]
